# 51: Alolan Vulpix

"Kah-kah—"

Seeing Gastly and Johan dare to block its way, Golbat got angry, letting out a furious cry.

At its command, the two Zubat instantly opened their mouths wide, unleashing "Supersonic."

"Ka-kah!"

Gastly cackled, and the black mist around its body began to swirl. A portion of the mist detached from Gastly, forming a dense cloud between itself and the sound waves.

The "Supersonic" vibrations passed harmlessly between the two wisps of fog, failing to disrupt Gastly at all!

In the next moment, Golbat saw two grinning Gastlys in front of it.

"Gastly, make it quick! It's badly injured and needs to get to the Pokémon Center immediately!"

"Ka-kah!"

Gastly responded with a determined nod.

Meanwhile, crouched on the ground, Johan ignored the filthy state of the small creature, trying to press his hand over its wound. Blood oozed between his fingers.

"Oowuu~~"

The little creature whimpered and struggled, clearly resisting Johan's touch.

Johan softened his voice. "I'm not a bad person."

The words seemed to trigger something in the little Pokémon, and its struggles intensified. As it thrashed, its wound reopened, and blood pooled on the ground.

It let out a trembling, frightened growl, baring its tiny fangs in an attempt to look fierce.

In the moonlight, its crystal-blue eyes, resembling polished gemstones, reflected fear—and Johan's gentle smile. The sight overlapped with something in its memory, leaving it confused.

But it also recalled a night when someone had given it the best meal it had ever tasted.

The little Pokémon's struggles eased, just slightly.

Johan saw the hesitation in the Vulpix's eyes and felt a weight settle in his heart.

Another Pokémon hurt and abandoned by humans…

Vulpix wasn't an uncommon species, and given its cuteness, it was highly sought after—second only to Pokémon like Eevee and Pichu.

Many owners kept Vulpix as a domestic pet.

But some more superstitious people feared it.

Because of ancient legends about the nine-tailed fox, some people viewed Vulpix and Ninetales as sinister creatures.

Judging by this little one's temperament, it hadn't been a very suitable "pet" to begin with.

Johan thought of a certain patient he once had.

Someone who wanted to build an "ideal society"—a "mathematical magician."

To a degree, Johan could understand his ideals.

"Ka-kah!"

Gastly floated over, having already dealt with the two Zubat. Golbat lay unconscious on the ground, minus a couple of teeth.

Johan didn't fuss over the missing teeth and instead gave a quick command to Gastly, "Put it to sleep with Hypnosis."

Johan's own hypnotic techniques required cooperation or catching the subject off guard. But this Vulpix was on high alert and injured, so Gastly's direct approach was necessary.

"Ka-kah~~"

Gastly nodded, its eyes glowing with the blue light of Hypnosis.

"Oowuu…"

Vulpix tried to resist, but Gastly's Hypnosis quickly overcame its defenses. After a few weak attempts, the little creature fell into a deep sleep.

Johan sighed.

He took off his jacket and wrapped the dirty little Pokémon in it before picking it up.

So much lighter than I expected…

"Let's go."

He hailed a taxi and headed straight to the Pokémon Center.

At the Rustboro Pokémon Center, Johan sat on a bench outside the emergency room, frowning as he waited.

"Ka-kah, Ka-kah~~"

Gastly floated back and forth, looking more anxious than Johan.

"Calm down, Gastly. We're at the Pokémon Center; it should be fine now," Johan tried to reassure it.

"Ka-kah?"

Do you have enough money?

Johan blinked in surprise. "You're worried about the money?"

"It's enough, don't worry."

"Not enough?" came a gentle voice from Johan's pants pocket.

Huh?

Johan took out his phone, realizing that he had only locked the screen earlier and hadn't actually hung up.

He lifted the phone to his ear, sighing.

"Uh, hey…ma'am…"

Just as he was about to say something—

[Your account has been credited with 200,000 Pokédollars. Current balance: 235,000 Pokédollars]

"…Has anyone ever told you that your voice sounds like a Lapras?" Johan said, his tone suddenly overly sweet.

"Ka-kah?"

Gastly watched Johan's abrupt shift in expression, cold sweat dripping down its face.

It felt like it had just witnessed something it shouldn't have.

The voice on the other end chuckled softly before asking, "Is that enough? If not, I can wake up my uncle."

"No, no, it's enough," Johan said, smiling into the phone while glaring at Gastly.

Click—

The emergency room door opened, and Nurse Joy emerged, pushing a cart covered in a white sheet.

"Ka-Kah!!"

Gastly gasped in horror.

A white sheet…

Did it die?!

Johan rolled his eyes. "Relax, it's just sleeping on top of the sheet."

Sure enough, Gastly saw the pristine white-furred creature lying peacefully on the soft white blanket, its six translucent, fluffy tails draped around it, breathing steadily.

"Ka-Kah?!"

Gastly's eyes widened in disbelief.

Is this the same little black ball from earlier?

It was hard to reconcile the snowy Vulpix in front of it with the dirty, matted creature it had rescued.

To be fair, Johan was just as astonished as Gastly.

He had known it was a Vulpix, but he hadn't expected it to be an Alolan Vulpix!

Or rather, an Ice-type Vulpix from Alola!

How did an Alolan Vulpix end up in Hoenn?

And in such a dire condition, no less.

"Mr. Johan, the little one is stable now, but it's severely malnourished and has lost a lot of blood. It's very weak, so it'll need proper care and nourishment."

Nurse Joy knew Johan by name since he often assisted the Rustboro Police Department in calming down rampaging Pokémon, and she frequently helped with these cases.

"Thank you."

Johan then went to settle the bill.

To his surprise, he learned that although he wasn't a registered Trainer, because of his consistent support of the League's Investigation Bureau and the Rustboro Police Department, he had long been granted free access to Pokémon Center services.

That saved quite a bit of money.

On the taxi ride home, Johan held the slumbering Alolan Vulpix in his arms. It looked like a delicate porcelain doll, its face serene as it slept.

"You must have been through a lot…"

The Vulpix instinctively snuggled deeper into Johan's arms, its nose twitching as if dreaming of something.

"Ka-Kah, Ka-Kah…"

Gastly watched the little creature, licking its lips.

Heh, an Alolan Vulpix… Heh heh…

Back home, Johan carefully laid the Alolan Vulpix on his bed, managing to find a leaf-patterned blanket from who-knows-where.

"Don't scare her; she's a girl," he reminded Gastly, who was hovering nearby, eyes gleaming with curiosity.

"Ka-kah?"

Gastly looked puzzled.

How do you know that?

Of course, Johan had already confirmed it earlier.

But he didn't bother explaining, heading out to the balcony with his phone.

Meanwhile, Gastly positioned itself protectively beside the sleeping Vulpix.

If Johan hadn't forbidden it, it would've licked her at least a hundred times by now.

"Still awake?"

On the balcony, moonlight poured down, casting a gentle, bright glow.

"Yeah," came the quiet, sleepy murmur from the other end of the line.

It seemed like she had already drifted off to sleep.

Johan decided to put off returning the "treatment fee" for now.

Returning inside, he found Gastly already dozing off beside the Vulpix, its mouth open, snoring lightly and occasionally mumbling, "Ka-kah" in its sleep.

Heh heh, an Alolan Vulpix…

Meanwhile, in a distant bedroom, golden-haired Cynthia was curled up among a pile of adorable plush dolls.

Moonlight filtered through the window, enveloping her in a soft, ethereal glow as she slept, a peaceful smile on her face.

Kindness should always be met with kindness.

# 52: Complete?

The next morning...

"Ka-kah!!"

Johan was startled awake by Gastly's panicked cries. He opened his sleepy eyes to see Gastly darting around the room like a headless chicken.

"Waking up without being numbed by a lick feels... almost strange," Johan muttered, his hand instinctively reaching to his bedside table for a Cheri Berry.

"What's wrong? Did you lose your learning machine?" he asked.

"Ka-kah Ka-kah!" Gastly responded, calling out anxiously.

The little fox is gone!

Huh?

Johan suddenly remembered the new addition to their home—a Pokémon.

He glanced towards the foot of the bed, but there was no sign of the Alolan Vulpix.

"Don't panic," he reassured Gastly. "We're on the sixth floor; it's not like it jumped out of a window. The door's locked, so it's got to be somewhere in the apartment."

With that, Johan hurried out of bed and began searching the apartment alongside Gastly.

"Ka-kah!!"

Luckily, Gastly's ability to phase through walls and furniture paid off. It finally found the little Vulpix huddled under the corner of a wardrobe.

The Vulpix had curled up tightly against the wall, hiding its head beneath its fluffy tail, with only two crystal-blue eyes peeking out.

Seeing Johan crouch down to peer at it and Gastly's mischievous grin, the Vulpix let out a warning growl.

"Oowu!!"

The little Pokémon bared its two tiny fangs, growling at them in a low, defensive tone.

Adorably fierce.

"Ka-kah~~"

Gastly's eyes sparkled with delight.

So cute~~

Johan, however, felt a weight settle in his chest.

The Alolan Vulpix's wariness is even worse than I thought...

It likely had a lot to do with whatever it had been through.

After considering for a moment, Johan grabbed a handful of Pokéblocks, crouched down, and extended his hand into the wardrobe's shadows.

Even as a Pokémon psychologist, gaining the trust of a hurt Pokémon still required basic communication and interaction.

And without the Vulpix using any abilities or moves, he couldn't read its Pokédex entry to learn more about it.

As the Vulpix sniffed the aroma of the Pokéblocks, its blue eyes flicked warily between Johan and the offered food.

"Go on, eat," Johan said with a gentle smile. "You lost a lot of blood yesterday, and you're still very weak. Eat something to get your strength back."

"Ka-kah Ka-kah~~"

Gastly encouraged the Vulpix as well, swallowing its own saliva.

Pokéblocks are so delicious. There's no way you can resist!

The Alolan Vulpix stared at the Pokéblocks for a moment.

Whap!

Suddenly, it swatted Johan's hand away with its paw, scattering the blocks across the floor.

"Hiss—"

Johan winced, three scratch marks appearing on his hand as fresh blood welled up from the wounds.

The Vulpix's eyes briefly showed a flash of guilt and distress before its expression hardened again.

"Ka-kah!"

Gastly hurriedly picked up each Pokéblock with great care, blowing on them gently, as if trying to make them less dirty.

Its eyes held a confused glint.

This makes no sense. Pokéblocks are so tasty!

Gastly couldn't help but compare this moment to its own recruitment—Johan had only needed a single cup of instant ramen to win it over.

The Vulpix's blue eyes watched Gastly's movements through the narrow gap beneath the wardrobe.

Johan leaned against the side of the wardrobe and slowly sat down, ignoring the pain from his scratches.

He was long used to minor injuries thanks to Gastly's antics, and this was no different.

"I remember..." he began, almost talking to himself.

"I once had a friend, a lot like you. He ended up all alone in a foreign place—a new city, a new school. He was afraid to get close to anyone and couldn't afford to fall behind in his studies."

"He loved Pokémon but didn't dare raise one. He had no time, no energy, and no money. So he chose to study Pokémon psychology because he thought... the hearts of Pokémon were simpler than people's."

To others, the original Johan seemed impressive.

He entered Silversilver Academy with the highest theory scores in all of the Hoenn region. He even graduated early after finishing the coursework well ahead of schedule, only a few years older than Steven and the others.

But no one knew how much he had sacrificed to achieve those grades and how much time he had given up.

"Ka-kah Ka-kah..."

Gastly, who had returned to Johan's side without him noticing, was listening intently, its expression complicated.

It was the first time it had ever heard Johan share this story.

Hearing about someone else's struggles, even when spoken of calmly, could always bring to mind one's own experiences.

"Oowu..."

A soft whimper came from beneath the wardrobe.

Johan and Gastly exchanged a look, sharing a smile.

This time, Johan didn't crouch down. Instead, he placed the Pokéblocks on a small dish and gently slid it under the wardrobe.

"Here, have something to eat," he offered. "When you've recovered, if you want to go home, I'll take you back to Alola. But if you want to stay..."

Before he could finish speaking, Johan felt a cold, soft tongue lick the wound on his hand.

The sensation was icy and gentle.

"Ka-kah~~"

"We're back—"

Before Johan had even opened the door, Gastly phased through it and eagerly searched the apartment for the Alolan Vulpix.

It caught a glimpse of the little white Pokémon scampering away.

"Looks like she's regained some energy," Johan observed as he walked in, carrying a bag.

After a day of gentle reassurance, the Alolan Vulpix had become less fearful of Johan and Gastly. Considering her weakened state, Johan had taken Gastly out shopping at the department store that evening.

Returning home, Johan saw Gastly waiting by the wardrobe once more.

It was clear where the Vulpix had hidden again.

Johan sighed inwardly.

Pokémon may have simple hearts, but they were still intelligent creatures capable of thought.

In some ways, their hearts were both resilient and fragile.

Resilient in how they could silently endure for countless years, keeping promises.

Fragile in how betrayal or abandonment could leave gaping wounds that might never heal.

"Vulpix, I bought you a gift," Johan said, sitting beside the wardrobe. "Would you like to see it?"

He pulled three light blue bows from his pocket.

"Oowu?"

The Alolan Vulpix poked her head out from under the wardrobe, eyeing the bows with bright, twinkling eyes.

"Can I put one on you?" Johan asked gently.

"Oowu..."

The Vulpix hesitated, then pulled her head back under the wardrobe.

"Ka-kah..."

Gastly drooped its head, disappointed.

Johan gave Gastly a reassuring smile.

Then, in a sudden blur of white, the Vulpix darted out from under the wardrobe and leaped straight into Johan's arms.

Thump!

The force of the jump knocked Johan flat on his back.

The Vulpix burrowed deep into Johan's embrace, her small body trembling slightly.

It seemed like she had made a big decision—and was feeling afraid and uncertain about it.

Sensing the Vulpix's nervousness, Johan smiled softly and gently cradled her in his arms.

He didn't say anything, just stroked her back slowly.

After a long while, the Vulpix finally began to calm down.

"Alright, let's get these on," Johan said, holding up the bows.

He tied one around her neck and placed the other two on her ears.

"All done! Take a look." Johan held up a mirror.

"Oowu?"

The Alolan Vulpix lifted her head hesitantly, her cheeks turning slightly pink.

"Ka-kah!!"

Gastly let out an excited howl, its tone almost wolf-like.

"They look great! I must have a good eye," Johan praised, smiling.

The Vulpix's blush deepened, and she shyly lowered her head.

Then, as if making another big decision, the little Pokémon leaned forward, gently licking Johan's cheek with her cool tongue.

"Ka-kah Ka-kah!"

Me too! Me too!

Gastly floated over, cackling gleefully, and stuck out its red tongue to lick Johan's other cheek.

Sluuurp—

One side of Johan's face began to frost over, while the other started to go numb.

Johan's mouth twitched.

Oddly... not too bad?

Up to this point, it seemed like everything was going perfectly.

However, Gastly was so excited that its tongue stretched out too far, splashing saliva onto one of Vulpix's new bows.

Stare!

In an instant, the Alolan Vulpix's entire demeanor changed.

Her eyes glowed with a faint blue light.

"Ka-Kah?! Σ(дlll)"

Wham!!

Gastly was sent flying, crashing hard against the wall.

Cold sweat dripped

down its face.

Psychic?!

Johan's pupils contracted.

He glanced at Gastly, now plastered against the wall.

Then he looked down at the Alolan Vulpix, who was blushing furiously and looking unsure of what had just happened.

It seems... she isn't fully aware of her abilities.

# 53: Riding Lance’s Coattails to Fame?

[Vulpix]

[Type: Ice]

[Ability: Snow Warning]

[Moves Known: Tail Whip, Powder Snow, Disable, Ice Shard, Grudge, Icy Wind, Confuse Ray, Hex, Aurora Beam, Psychic, Hypnosis, Baby-Doll Eyes]

[Mood: Happy, Hesitant (I'm a harbinger of misfortune...)]

[Condition: Weak (It needs supplements! Supplements!)]

[Affection Level: 70 (It acknowledges you, but that's not enough)]

When the Alolan Vulpix used Psychic, Johan was finally able to see its Pokédex entry.

Simply judging by the list of moves, it was clear that this Vulpix had learned almost as many moves as Gastly. However, the moves' colors on the screen appeared quite dull, indicating a lack of proficiency.

In fact, like the Psychic from earlier, the Alolan Vulpix likely wasn't even aware it could use the move.

This might have been because it used to live with its group, learning moves but rarely needing to battle on its own.

In reality, Alolan Vulpix are communal Pokémon, with their groups often protected by an older Ninetales.

After all, in the snowy mountains of Alola, Alolan Vulpix is a primary target for Weavile.

And judging by Vulpix's reaction earlier…

Could this little one, despite its cute appearance, actually be a hidden bundle of fury?

Johan couldn't help but feel a pang of worry for his small household.

Still, he smiled and pulled out a Luxury Ball as he spoke.

"Vulpix…"

Before he could finish, the Vulpix curiously tapped the button on the Luxury Ball with its paw.

A flash of red light.

"Click—"

There wasn't even a hint of struggle; the Luxury Ball settled with an almost anticlimactic stillness.

"Ah, well," Johan sighed dramatically. "I wanted at least a little excitement…"

"Ka-kah~~"

Gastly floated back, its demeanor a little shaken.

Did we get tricked by its cute and weak appearance?

Johan shot Gastly a sidelong glance.

Who was the one going "So cute!" and "Hehehe" just a moment ago?

Gastly chuckled sheepishly.

Well, it is cute…

"She probably doesn't even realize her own strength," Johan mused as he held the Luxury Ball in his hand. "From now on, let's live together."

The Luxury Ball shook slightly in response.

Gastly grinned broadly, delighted to finally have a partner to spar with!

Come to think of it, the Alolan Vulpix shared quite a few moves with Gastly, like Confuse Ray and Hex...

"Thinking about it…" Johan murmured as he thoughtfully rubbed the ball in his hand.

A centuries-old ghost and a fox spirit...

Sounds like a setup for a mystical duo.

That night…

Gastly enthusiastically showed the young Vulpix around the apartment.

"Ka-kah~~"

"This is the kitchen. Johan doesn't let me in here, but between you and me…"

Gastly lowered its voice conspiratorially.

"…you can sneak in sometimes."

Gastly puffed up with pride.

"Oowu?"

The Alolan Vulpix blinked its eyes.

Why would you want to go in?

"Ka-kah—"

"To secretly inhale gas, of course! I've got a technique: as long as you don't take too much at once, Johan won't notice!"

"Oowu?! (・・)"

Gastly then led the young Vulpix to another room.

"Ka-kah~~"

"This is the bathroom, where you can take baths."

Gastly flashed a mischievous grin.

"…together, hehehe…"

"Oowu!!"

Startled, the Vulpix's latent psychic power surged.

"Ka—kah——"

Elsewhere, Johan received an unexpected phone call.

"Lance?"

"Is there still something wrong with Aerodactyl?"

From the other end of the line came Lance's somewhat awkward voice.

"Ahem, no, Aerodactyl's fine. Especially after listening to your 'hypnotic audio reinforcement'... Johan, well, there's a new issue. It's not really an issue, just..."

Johan frowned.

"Lance, since when did you get so hesitant? This doesn't seem like you."

"Well... alright, alright, the problem is me. The video of our battle got uploaded online," Lance admitted.

Huh?!

Johan stood up from his chair.

"...The 'body cam' wasn't turned off. The video was automatically streamed to the control room. Normally, it wouldn't be an issue, since nobody watches those unless we're on a mission. But, as luck would have it, Agatha happened to see it..."

"And she said, 'This is what a battle should look like—this is tactical synergy at its finest.' So…"

Johan silently picked up his phone and logged into the official League site.

Sure enough, the video was on the homepage, recorded from Lance's perspective. The title read:

"Dirty? Skillful? Don't Use Such Words When Talking About Tactics"

It had already accumulated over 100,000 views and was rising by the second.

There were plenty of comments below.

[BurningLeaf]: "Tried it myself—yep, it's dirty—err, I mean strong! Maybe I just wasn't skilled enough, or my opponent was too strong, but I lost. Still, it gave me new insights into battle strategies."

[MaskedMan]: "Is this an evolution of the 'Toxic Stall' strategy?"

[FactoryHead]: "Rental battle? Such coordination with borrowed Pokémon is rare."

And many, many more.

Johan rubbed his forehead.

He could already feel the ripples this video would create.

His research paper's popularity seemed to be picking up traction again.

More importantly, his online consultation room had received a sudden surge of new "patients."

He had been so preoccupied with helping the Alolan Vulpix all day that he hadn't paid attention to his phone.

"I'm really sorry, Johan," Lance said. "I only found out today. But Agatha said all revenue from the video would go to you, and she's willing to send you a rare item or a move-learning machine as compensation."

Lance continued, "Of course, if you think this has negatively impacted you, I can contact them to take the video down right away."

"Ahem." Johan cleared his throat as he glanced at the new balance in his bank account.

"You know, Lance, this video is more impactful on you," Johan pointed out.

After all, this practically meant he was gaining fame at the expense of the renowned Dragon Tamer.

"Wins and losses are part of life. This battle taught me a lot. Everything else is secondary," Lance replied, laughing.

Johan was slightly taken aback by his easygoing attitude.

"Besides, my face isn't even in the video."

Johan blinked, dumbfounded.

Since the camera was mounted on Lance's shoulder, it was true that his face wasn't in the footage.

Of course, anyone determined enough could figure it out, but Lance's words seemed meant to ease Johan's concerns.

"It's a joke. Anyway, what do you want to do? It's your call," Lance said.

After mulling it over for a moment, Johan sighed.

"Leave it," he said.

The battle had been for treating Aerodactyl, and there was no need to keep it under wraps.

Besides, several of the gardeners and staff at the Stone estate had witnessed the match anyway.

And as far as gaining fame went… Johan figured it was inevitable. Why else would he have published that research paper?

Plus, with so many people seeking his consultation, he'd be able to help more people and Pokémon.

It might even bring in a bit of extra income.

And it would give him more material for his research.

He glanced over at Gastly and Vulpix, who were now playing together.

With an extra Pokémon to care for, his expenses were only going to increase.

After hanging up the call, Johan turned his attention to the messages in his consultation inbox.

Most of the simpler queries were answered quickly.

A few more complex ones remained.

For example—

[PsychicGirl]: "Why won't my Kadabra evolve?"

# 54: The Psychic Girl, Sabrina

In the game, evolving Kadabra into Alakazam is similar to evolving Haunter into Gengar, as both require a Trade Evolution.

Back before the introduction of the Link Cable item, evolving Pokémon like Haunter into its final form was a hurdle single-player fans struggled to overcome.

However, the situation is slightly different in the Pokémon world.

That's because "trading" essentially replicates the special magnetic fields found in the wild, which are necessary to stimulate certain Pokémon to complete their evolution.

This explains why wild Alakazam, Gengar, and Golem are seen in the wild.

To be honest, questions like these shouldn't be directed at someone like Johan, a Pokémon psychologist. Although not widely known, this is general knowledge.

Still, after some thought, Johan decided to respond.

[Grain in the Field]: You could try using the Link Cable item or attempt Trade Evolution.

The response came faster than expected.

[Psychic Girl]: If that worked, why would I be asking you?

Johan: "??"

To be honest, this was the first time he had encountered someone with such a short temper.

Birds of every feather, huh.

Johan patted his cheeks to snap himself out of his "keyboard warrior" mindset. He had a hunch that the person on the other end had quite a bad temper. If he butted heads with her...

Things could easily escalate into a "doctor-patient dispute."

However, her words did make him think more deeply.

Typically, Pokémon like Kadabra or Haunter evolve immediately through the use of a Link Cable or Trade Evolution methods.

But from what she was saying, it seemed like she had already tried those methods without success.

Since this world's reality can't follow game mechanics to the letter…

[Grain in the Field]: Can you describe Kadabra's current state or tell me what it usually does?

[Psychic Girl]: What else can it do? (eye-roll emoji) It just meditates, spaces out, and meditates again.

[Grain in the Field]: What about its eating and sleeping habits?

[Psychic Girl]: ??? Do Psychic Pokémon need to sleep? Do you even know what you're talking about?

Johan: "…"

Taking a deep breath, Johan forced a smile.

[Grain in the Field]: Well, I think you should try asking Kadabra what it wants. Even though it's a Psychic-type, it's not a machine. It has its own thoughts, routines, and preferences. It can't just be stuck meditating all the time.

[Grain in the Field]: Try adding some color to its life.

He wanted to add more, but noticed that the user had gone offline!

"Ka-kah?"

Gastly seemed to notice Johan's mood, floating over curiously.

"It's nothing," Johan replied, waving it off with a smile.

"Ka-kah~~"

Gastly grinned and floated away to find Alolan Vulpix to play with.

So, it's that whole "Psychic superiority complex" thing, huh? Johan thought darkly. Maybe Mewtwo should give them all a reality check!

Dangerous thoughts flickered in his eyes as he headed for the bathroom.

After splashing his face with cold water, Johan stared at his reflection in the mirror.

My emotions are all over the place… It seems that taking in Vulpix has put an invisible strain on me.

This kind of mental state wasn't acceptable for a Pokémon psychologist and trainer. Not only could it impact himself, but it could also affect his Pokémon.

Luckily, he realized it in time.

Kanto Region—Saffron City

As the most prosperous city in Kanto, Saffron is home to the Pokémon world's corporate giant, Silph Co., as well as two powerful gyms.

Deep within the Psychic-type Gym lies a room cluttered with a multitude of plushies—some shaped like people and others resembling various Pokémon.

Seated among these plushies is a girl in her late teens, wearing a form-fitting red outfit and long, straight hair.

She is Sabrina, the Psychic-type Gym Leader.

Standing before her is her Kadabra.

"Kadabra, do you need sleep?" Sabrina asked flatly, pressing a hand to her forehead.

Before Kadabra could answer, Sabrina's eyes began to glow with an eerie blue light.

In sync, Kadabra's eyes reflected the same blue glow.

With a slight shake of Sabrina's head, Kadabra awkwardly mirrored her movement.

Seeing this, Sabrina smiled softly and murmured:

"See? Psychic Pokémon don't need to sleep."

When the blue light in Sabrina's eyes faded, Kadabra returned to normal. A flicker of resistance crossed its eyes but quickly vanished.

"Still…"

Sabrina tilted her head slightly, as if reconsidering.

"…it couldn't hurt to try, right?"

Kadabra blinked, a hint of surprise showing in its gaze as it glanced at Sabrina.

Adjusting his mindset, Johan exited the bathroom and spotted Gastly and Alolan Vulpix seemingly getting along, laughing together.

Yet, the more he listened, the more something felt off.

He saw Gastly lead Alolan Vulpix to a large mirror.

"Ka-kah~~"

"Let me tell you something. In our family, the most important skill is Hypnosis."

"Oowu?"

Vulpix tilted its head, not fully understanding, but nodded earnestly.

Pleased, Gastly nodded back.

"Ka-kah Ka-kah!"

"Next is… Transformation!"

Huh? Johan, pouring himself a glass of water, listened curiously.

Gastly's first lesson seemed reasonable enough. As the Pokémon of a psychologist, mastering Hypnosis wasn't a bad idea.

Alolan Vulpix had already learned Hypnosis, and judging by the strength of its Psychic, it seemed to have considerable psychic abilities to build on.

But what was this about "Transformation"?

Johan watched as Gastly's black fog began to shift and change, its appearance morphing.

"Ka-kah!"

"Now I'm… Gastly-Belle!"

"Oowu!!"

Alolan Vulpix's eyes sparkled with admiration.

Gastly smirked, the black fog shifting again under Vulpix's expectant gaze.

"Ka-kah!"

"Now I'm… Ghost-Tina!"

Pffft—

Johan nearly spat out his water.

"Oowu—"

Vulpix gazed in wonder, its expression more admiring than ever.

Gastly is amazing!

To be fair, ever since Gastly learned Substitute, its shape-shifting abilities had indeed improved.

"Ka-ka-ka~~"

Gastly couldn't stop grinning.

It decided to keep pushing its "elder sibling" advantage.

"Ka-kah~~"

"And after that, the most important thing… is scaring people, attacking from behind!"

Johan quickly put down his glass and picked up the soft and squishy Alolan Vulpix.

"It's time for bed."

If he let Gastly keep teaching, he feared the young Vulpix might pick up some very questionable habits.

"Oowu~~"

Being suddenly scooped up, Alolan Vulpix blushed slightly, letting out a low whimper before burying its head into Johan's chest.

Johan couldn't resist reaching out and running his fingers through the fluffy fur on its head, a contented smile spreading across his face.

The stress he'd been feeling slowly melted away.

Turns out, nothing relieves stress better than petting a cat… or rather, an Alolan Vulpix.

"So soft and cool to the touch. This is the good stuff."

"Oowu~~"

Vulpix purred softly, making a sound similar to a cat's purr.

"Oowu!"

"Ka-kah?"

Seeing Alolan Vulpix being whisked away mid-lesson, Gastly was stunned.

It hurriedly floated after them.

Wait! I haven't finished teaching yet!

"Whether Vulpix wants to grow stronger, train, or battle, that's up to her," Johan's voice called back.

Gastly nodded thoughtfully.

That makes sense…

Then it paused.

Wait a minute, didn't I already teach that lesson?!

"Gastly, turn off the lights."

Click!

Relieved that Johan hadn't caught on, Gastly happily floated towards the bed.

"Ka-kah—"

"I'm coming too—"

But after the lights went out, neither Johan nor Gastly noticed…

In the large mirror they had been standing in front of earlier, a pair of crimson eyes blinked open, filled with confusion.

"Where's the login failure?"

# 55: Taking That First Step

"Ka-kah~~"

Gastly floated inside, casually opening the door for Johan, who had just reached the door with keys in hand. Johan felt a bit awkward.

Johan's eyebrow twitched.

"Gastly, next time, wait until the door's open before going inside."

"Ka-kah?"

Gastly turned around with a puzzled look.

Why?

Johan took a deep breath, "Didn't you see the notice? There are warnings about burglars in the neighborhood. If people see you phasing through doors and unlocking them, we might get mistaken for thieves."

"Ka-kah?! Σ(дlll)"

Seeing Gastly's shocked expression, Johan nodded in satisfaction and closed the door.

"Understand now?"

"Ka-kah?"

As Johan walked away, Gastly's eyes sparkled.

It really was like that! Gastly hadn't noticed it before.

But now... heh-heh—

"Oowu?"

Hearing the commotion, Alolan Vulpix peeked cautiously around the doorframe. Upon seeing Johan and Gastly, her eyes lit up.

She happily darted towards Johan.

"Oowu Oowu—"

"Wait, wait, Vulpix—"

Thud!

Before Johan could finish, Alolan Vulpix had pounced on him, licking his face affectionately.

Wiping the frost off his face, Johan shot an annoyed glance at Gastly, who was laughing heartily nearby.

He picked up the little one and set her down.

Despite Alolan Vulpix's small size, with her fluffy fur making her appear not much bigger than a kitten, her strength was no joke.

She had no problem knocking over an adult like Johan with ease.

However, what surprised Johan the most was Vulpix's psychic power. It was completely unexpected for an Ice-type Pokémon.

Like right now.

As he tried to stand up, he felt himself being lifted by a gentle but firm psychic force.

Is she really an Ice-type Pokémon? Or is this Psyduck reincarnated?

"But you're recovering well," Johan noted.

This was the third day since he had taken in Alolan Vulpix, and in the Pokédex information he could see, her [Status] had improved from "Weak" to "Good."

"There's a fireworks show tonight. Let's get ready, and we'll head out soon," Johan said, patting her soft fur.

"Oowu, oowu?"

Hearing this, Alolan Vulpix hesitated and backed away a few steps, showing signs of anxiety and fear in her eyes.

This was her current state—besides being unaware of her own strength and possibly having a "hidden violent streak," she was also incredibly shy and lacked confidence.

"Don't worry, we'll go together," Johan reassured her gently.

"Oowu, oowu…"

With Johan's calming words, the little one eventually agreed and turned to start packing up with Gastly.

Johan hadn't pried too deeply into Vulpix's past.

For one, the pain of revisiting those memories might be too much. It had taken a lot for her to start opening up, and there was no need to risk reopening old wounds.

Additionally, Johan had been in touch with Officer Jenny at the Rustboro police department to inquire about any illegal trades involving Alolan Pokémon. If results came from there, Vulpix wouldn't have to recall her own trauma.

Still, he had managed to learn from indirect questioning that Vulpix had indeed been abandoned.

However, she had never been officially captured; it seemed like whoever had taken her in had intended to keep her as a pet.

In Johan's eyes, Vulpix's past was important, but if he had to choose, her future mattered more.

If she remained stuck in the past, she'd never take that first step forward.

She needed to take that first step.

Half an hour later.

The streets of Rustboro were bustling, with Alolan Vulpix perched on Johan's head, gripping his hair tightly.

"Vulpix, if you keep holding on like this, I'm going to end up bald."

"Oowu!"

Realizing her mistake, Vulpix quickly loosened her grip and blew on Johan's scalp as if to soothe it.

Cr-cr-crack.

A thin layer of frost formed.

Johan: "…"

He wondered if, aside from Chesto Berries and Pecha Berries, he should start carrying some Aspear Berries.

**[Aspear Berry: Cures freezing or frostbite.]**

"Ka-kah, Ka-kah~~"

In contrast to Vulpix, Gastly was much more carefree.

Especially since it had somehow dug out a big red bow from home and tied it around its head.

Gastly proudly called it a "Vulpix-matching style."

If Johan hadn't stopped him, Gastly would've already been showing it off to strangers, asking if they liked his new look.

One was a social butterfly; the other was socially anxious. Quite the pair.

"Uncle, that Vulpix is so cute! Why is it white?" asked a little girl, around eight or nine years old, holding a Jigglypuff plushie. Her eyes sparkled with curiosity.

Uncle?

"Oowu!"

Alolan Vulpix trembled and dug her claws into Johan's hair again, burying her head.

"Hiss—"

Johan took a sharp breath, forcing a smile. "C-call me big brother."

"Okay, Uncle. Is that really a Vulpix?" The little girl tilted her head cutely.

Just then—

Gastly appeared out of nowhere and made a goofy face, sticking out its tongue.

"Ka-kah!!"

For a moment, the little girl froze, her eyes filling with tears.

Oh no, oh no!

Johan quickly grabbed Gastly and ran, spotting the girl's parents nearby.

After a short jog, they arrived at the riverside park, the venue for the fireworks show. A cheerful crowd had already gathered, chatting and laughing.

Johan found a quiet patch of grass off to the side to sit on, with Vulpix and Gastly beside him.

"Ka-kah?" Gastly pouted.

Why'd you pull me away?

Didn't you see that girl found me so cute, she wanted to cry?

Johan's brow furrowed.

Yeah, cute to death.

"Vulpix."

"Oowu?"

"Didn't you notice? Along the way, a lot of people were complimenting you, saying you were cute and beautiful."

"Oowu?"

Really?

"If you just lift your head and stand tall, you'll find that you're much more popular than you think."

Johan gently set Vulpix down on the grass.

He touched his hair.

Good grief.

It was rock solid.

But now wasn't the time to worry about that.

He patted Vulpix's head softly and said:

"Take a step forward. Learn from Gastly—"

Turning to look, he saw Gastly had wandered over to a lovey-dovey couple, making exaggerated faces and showing off its big red bow.

The startled couple took off running.

Johan: "…"

"Uh, don't learn from Gastly. Just be yourself. Take a step forward, and maybe you'll see a whole new view."

"Oowu…"

Encouraged by Johan's words, Vulpix steeled herself and closed her eyes tightly.

Plip, plip. She took a few hesitant steps forward.

Boom!

Suddenly, a dazzling firework lit up the night sky.

The night was illuminated, as if scattered with stars.

Startled, Vulpix instinctively opened her eyes.

And saw the brilliant display of fireworks.

The colors painted the sky, reflecting on her pure white fur and lighting up her crystal-clear eyes.

"Oowu~~"

So beautiful.

Johan gently picked her up, with Gastly floating back beside him.

The three of them sat on the grass, watching the bright flowers in the sky bloom and unconsciously smiling.

"Make a wish on the fireworks. It's supposed to be really lucky," Johan said.

"Ka-kah?"

"Oowu?"

Really?

Gastly and Vulpix exchanged a look.

"Ka-kah—"

Gastly cackled.

My wish? To help Johan and Little Vulpix conquer Champions and dominate Elites!

"Oowu~~"

Vulpix blinked softly, smiling to herself.

Gastly says Johan's destined to become an amazing trainer. I want to keep up!

They both turned to look at Johan.

"Well, me? The world's so big—I just want to take you both to see it!" Johan laughed.

Boom!

A golden Pikachu-shaped firework exploded in the sky.

# 56: The Invitation

Night had fallen deeply.

After the fireworks show, Johan carried a sleeping Alolan Vulpix, accompanied by Gastly, as they headed home.

Along the way, his phone suddenly buzzed.

He pulled it out and saw—

[Psychic Girl]: You've got some skills.

[Grain in the Field]: ?

[Psychic Girl]: Kadabra didn't evolve, but after a nap, its psychic power grew significantly!

Girl, did you sleep for three days?

How long has it been since you last slept?

This exchange confirmed Johan's suspicion that "Psychic Girl" was most likely a training fanatic. Her Pokémon probably barely had any time to rest.

[Psychic Girl]: Got any other suggestions? If Kadabra evolves or its psychic powers improve further, we're going to challenge it!

Challenge it?

Another Pokémon?

This confirmed she was either a training or battle maniac.

And trainers like this often lacked essential emotional bonds or connections with their Pokémon.

This could explain why her Kadabra hadn't evolved despite meeting all the conditions—it likely had something to do with "Psychic Girl" herself.

[Grain in the Field]: Maybe you two should try doing something else? Like watching a movie together, going on a nature walk, or…cooking a meal together.

[Psychic Girl]: ?? What nonsense is this?

[Grain in the Field]: Just try it. And listen to what Kadabra has to say.

Johan had realized something.

With people who are arrogant and ill-tempered, you don't need to be overly polite. Otherwise, they'll end up walking all over you.

But Johan soon thought about his own Alolan Vulpix.

Her psychic powers were quite strong, but she had poor control over them.

[Grain in the Field]: By the way, you seem really skilled at training Psychic-type Pokémon. Do you have any methods to help them improve their psychic control?

[Psychic Girl]: What? You can't even control basic psychic abilities? (Mocking.gif)

Johan ignored her condescending remarks.

[Grain in the Field]: You're not saying you don't have any, right?

[Psychic Girl]: Don't be ridiculous! Just wait!

After a while, as Johan neared home, his phone buzzed again.

She had sent a file—

Training Notes for Psychic Pokémon.

[Psychic Girl]: Old man's notes. Took forever to find them.

After sending this, her profile went offline.

But Johan's eyes lit up.

She actually had something!

Although Alolan Vulpix was an Ice-type Pokémon, approaching her training from the perspective of psychic power was not a bad idea.

After his experience with "Psychic Girl," Johan made a decision:

He needed to set a friend request approval requirement—applications only, no automatic adds.

It wasn't about his rising popularity going to his head; it was more about the odd and random questions some people sent that left him bewildered.

For example:

Someone once asked him, "Who's stronger, Magikarp or Feebas?"

And another asked, "Hey, do you get pregnant from kissing? (Gardevoir-blushing.gif)"

Humans really can't—or, at least, shouldn't—think this way.

Such cases were just the tip of the iceberg.

If he didn't set a filter, he'd be dealing with mental gymnastics every single day.

At the very least, questions should be relevant to Pokémon.

Looking up at the bright moon, Johan shook his hands to relax.

Tired.

Carrying Alolan Vulpix was no easy task either.

"Ka-kah?"

Gastly floated over with a cheeky grin.

Need me to take over?

Johan squinted at him suspiciously. Before he could respond, his phone buzzed again.

[Eevee Eats Cabbage]: Time for meditation.

Johan sighed heavily.

"No rest for the weary…"

[Grain in the Field]: Give me a moment, I'll be right there!

The next morning.

Johan woke up feeling remarkably refreshed despite the icy and tingling sensations. After a quick wash and a rushed breakfast, he headed to the academy.

But today, he brought Alolan Vulpix along.

Keeping her locked inside all the time wouldn't do her any good.

And last night, she had shown great progress.

She had bravely taken a step forward.

As the coffee machine loudly brewed his cup, Johan prepared himself a fresh coffee.

"Vulpix."

Hearing Johan call, the somewhat nervous and lost-looking Alolan Vulpix turned towards him.

She saw Johan smiling at her while holding a cup of coffee and crouching down.

Gastly's expression turned alarmed.

"Ka-kah!"

Don't drink it!

But Johan simply chuckled, "Blow on it."

"Oowu?"

Alolan Vulpix wasn't sure what was going on, but she obediently blew on the cup.

In an instant, the steaming hot espresso turned into a chilled iced coffee.

Johan took a big gulp, smacking his lips in satisfaction, and patted Vulpix's head in praise.

"Well done, Vulpix."

"Oowu~~"

Vulpix bashfully turned her head away.

It's really nothing…

But the way her six fluffy tails swayed in excitement gave away her true feelings.

"Ka-kah?!"

Gastly, who was floating mid-air, froze.

Wait, hold up!

Aren't you gonna let Vulpix have a sip?!

"Gastly, I have a morning meeting today. Look after Vulpix and make sure she doesn't wander off."

After finishing his iced coffee in one go, Johan gave his instructions.

"Ka-kah~~"

Gastly nodded solemnly.

Mission accepted!

Bang.

As soon as Johan left, Gastly grinned mischievously and turned to Alolan Vulpix.

"Oowu?"

Seeing Gastly's expression, Vulpix's body tensed slightly, with faint psychic ripples emanating.

"Ka-kah, Ka-kah!"

Gastly quickly patted his face and dialed back his grin, clearing his throat.

"Ka-kah~~"

"As I mentioned before, in the Johan household, 'Hypnosis' is the most important skill. Now, let me teach you the hypnosis techniques Johan once taught me."

Hearing this, Alolan Vulpix's eyes sparkled with excitement, and she nodded eagerly.

After the morning meeting.

Duf, the first-year battle class advisor who had become acquainted with Johan during their previous field trip, approached him.

"Mr. Duf, this isn't appropriate."

"Of course it is, Professor Johan!" the well-built Duf replied confidently. "Your battle video with Lance went viral online, and even the director praised it during the meeting…"

Duf, with his well-organized thoughts, was highly respected as the main advisor for the academy's most prominent first-year battle class.

"The kids in the first-year class idolize your strategies. If you give them a tactics lesson, they'd be overjoyed."

"But I'm just a Pokémon psychology instructor," Johan replied helplessly.

"Thanks, but no thanks."

"Don't worry, you won't be teaching alone. I've invited an old friend of mine to help. Even if you just make an appearance, it'll be more than enough."

Duf laughed heartily, clapping Johan on the shoulder before leaving without giving him a chance to refuse.

An old friend?

"Who is it?" Johan asked, curious.

"Royal Masked Man!" Duf's voice echoed down the hallway.

Huh?

Johan blinked in surprise.

Royal Masked Man?

Professor Kukui?!

Feeling a mix of confusion and anticipation, Johan returned to his consultation room.

As soon as he opened the door—

He shivered violently.

Inside the room, a thin layer of snow had formed, turning it into a winter wonderland.

Alolan Vulpix stood dazed in the middle of the room, while Gastly awkwardly turned his head, forcing a grin.

"Ka-ka-kah"

I-It's not my fault, I swear!

# 57: Teaching and Solving Mysteries

"You two…" Johan pinched the bridge of his nose, taking a deep breath.

It was evident what had caused the state of the consultation room—Alolan Vulpix's ability, Snow Warning.

[Snow Warning: When a Pokémon with this ability enters the field, the weather will change to 'Snow,' lasting for a set duration.]

This ability, along with Drought, Drizzle, and Sand Stream, is known as one of the four major weather abilities.

Experienced trainers often build their entire teams around such abilities, forming strategies like Hail Teams, Sun Teams, Rain Teams, and Sandstorm Teams.

For now, Johan wasn't aiming to form such a team. He had only two Pokémon and was finding it challenging enough to care for them, let alone create an entire team strategy. However, Alolan Vulpix's ability gave him a possible direction to explore.

Although Johan had been aware of Vulpix's ability, since he had captured her, she hadn't exhibited any characteristics of Snow Warning.

"Oowu…"

Alolan Vulpix tensed up, lowering her head as if realizing she had done something wrong.

"You two… wanna have a snowball fight?" Johan's voice cut through the tension.

"Ka-kah?"

"Oowu?"

Smack!

Before Gastly could react, a snowball splattered against its face, bursting into a flurry of snowflakes. The culprit was, of course, Johan, who crouched down, rolling a second snowball.

"Ka-kah!!"

Gastly, now aware of what was happening, let out a sinister laugh and began manipulating the accumulated snow with its black mist.

"Vulpix, quickly, hit it!" Johan urged.

Smack!

Just as he was speaking, Johan himself got nailed with a snowball square on the head, turning half his hair white.

"Oowu…"

Alolan Vulpix stood there, stunned, watching Johan and Gastly locked in a playful snowball fight. Her nose twitched slightly, and her eyes filled with tears.

In the past, her uncontrollable snowstorms and her inability to manage her abilities had led her to be deemed an ill omen.

That's why she always tried to suppress herself—so much so that even she forgot what she was capable of.

But Johan and Gastly didn't reject or hate her. They accepted her as she was.

"Oowu!"

With a deep breath, Alolan Vulpix focused her powers, quickly gathering a snowball. In an instant, it had grown to the size of a millstone.

"Ka-kah?! Σ(дlll)"

Seeing the massive snowball, Gastly yelped in shock.

Is she cheating?!

Johan's eyebrow twitched.

He turned his head and closed his eyes.

Gastly, tonight Vulpix will let you have two sips…

Boom—

"Ka-kah!!"

Two hours later.

Johan, having shed his jacket and rolled up his sleeves, wiped the sweat from his forehead. He had finally managed to clean up the snow that had piled up in the consultation room.

Gastly lay sprawled on a single-seater couch, pretending to be weak, while Alolan Vulpix lay beside it with an apologetic expression. She gently fanned Gastly with her six fluffy tails like a large fan.

Gastly, meanwhile, looked to be in heaven.

"Ka-kah~~ o(〃'▽'〃)o"

Johan chose not to call Gastly out on its act. After all, it had indeed been hit directly by Alolan Vulpix's "giant spiral snowball."

"Alright, get ready. We're heading out to help with a tactical class."

"Ka-kah?"

Gastly perked up.

Helping in class?

Johan nodded.

If it were just about assisting Duf with a tactical class, Johan would have refused.

After all, if he were to teach… well, his strategies could be a bit too intense for the students' innocent minds.

But since Duf had also invited the Royal Masked Man, Johan decided it was worth attending.

As a professional wrestler, the Royal Masked Man was quite popular online. However, that identity wasn't the key point.

The critical point was the person behind the mask—Professor Kukui, the future leading researcher of Pokémon moves in the Alola Region!

Of course, Professor Kukui might not yet have earned his doctorate at this stage; he was likely still studying and gaining experience.

This brought Johan's thoughts to the Alola region.

Alola hadn't established a Pokémon League yet, and one of Kukui's greatest dreams was to establish the Alola League.

To achieve this, the young Kukui was traveling and studying different regions to learn from their League systems. In the future, he would not only establish the Alola Pokémon League but also the Alola Pokémon School.

He would serve as both the League Champion and a teacher at the school while continuing his research on Pokémon moves—a remarkably capable individual.

He would be one of the rare few to achieve a doctorate and become a top-tier trainer.

The previous person to do so was Professor Oak.

Moreover, despite all his success, Kukui would eventually marry a very beautiful wife—Professor Burnet!

Not even Professor Oak could top that.

For Johan, though, the most important part was that Kukui was from Alola.

And Alolan Vulpix was also from Alola.

The largest lecture hall at Kanaz Academy, which also served as the academy's auditorium.

Students began arriving in groups, chatting happily and accompanied by their Pokémon.

Backstage in the preparation room, or more accurately, the dressing room behind the stage—

"Mr. Duf, didn't you say this was for the first-year battle class?" Johan rubbed his forehead.

Such a large lecture hall couldn't possibly be for just one class.

"Hahahaha—it's all good, it's all good," Duf laughed heartily.

He leaned in closer to Johan and whispered, "Don't worry, the academy approved this event, and Professor Johan, your 'appearance fee' is the same as the Royal Masked Man's."

He gave Johan a knowing look and a wink.

Johan straightened his expression.

"As an instructor, the 'appearance fee' isn't important. I simply believe that a teacher should focus on guidance, education, and problem-solving."

"Guidance, education, and problem-solving… well said!"

At that moment, a man with a well-built but not overly muscular frame walked in, wearing an exaggerated mask.

It was the Royal Masked Man, Kukui!

"Alola, you two!"

Seeing Duf and Johan looking over, Kukui grinned and posed with his hands on his hips.

It was very reminiscent of a certain teacher's iconic stance in the Pokémon world.

After exchanging greetings, Johan learned from Duf that he used to be a wrestler himself. He had lost a match to Kukui and challenged him to a Pokémon battle to prove himself. He lost again, but the two had become friends.

"Ka-kah!"

Gastly mimicked Kukui's pose, grinning widely.

Duf and Kukui both paused, surprised.

Johan turned away.

Can't bear to watch…

"Hahaha—looks like you two will get along just fine," Duf chuckled, pushing over a cart filled with Poké Balls.

"You guys can chat for now. You might need to demonstrate some tactics later, and these are for teaching purposes."

As an instructor in the battle class, Duf couldn't rely solely on his own Pokémon to demonstrate every tactic and concept. Therefore, teaching Pokémon were essential.

These Pokémon typically resided in the academy's dedicated care facilities. While they wouldn't be suitable for actual battles, they were perfect for demonstrations.

After Duf left, Kukui, ever friendly, turned to Johan.

"I watched your battle with the Dragon Master Lance, Professor Johan. Your strategy of Protect-Toxic Substitute was impressively executed, with additional layers of tactical thinking…"

I saw your comment too, Johan thought silently, smiling as he replied, "I often watch your matches as the Royal Masked Man, too."

"Oh, really? Which one?" Kukui's eyes lit up with curiosity.

Johan: "…"

"Hey, is this an Alolan Vulpix?" Kukui asked, his attention shifting to the little Pokémon nestled in Johan's arms.

"Oowu~"

Hearing Kukui's voice, Alolan Vulpix burrowed further into Johan's embrace.

"It is," Johan confirmed, gently stroking the little one's fur to reassure her.

"Mr. Royal Masked Man, you're from Alola, right? I'd like to ask you a few things after class if that's alright."

"No problem!" Kukui thumped his chest enthusiastically, his eyes burning with determination.

"But Professor Johan, don't hold back later—I want to see your tactics in action!"

Johan, who was browsing through the teaching Pokémon list, paused.

A slow smile spread across his face.

"Is that so?"

His hand stopped on a Poké Ball containing a Lapras.

Gastly, it looks like you've got a new strategy to try.

# 58: Lapras, Sing Your Song

"It's Johan-sensei!"

"And there's also the Royal Masked Man!"

"Wow—are these two really giving us a tactics class?"

The students buzzed with excitement as Johan and Kukui stepped onto the stage, introduced by Duf. Both of them were well-known figures, and their presence caused quite a stir among the first-year battle students who had gathered for a routine tactics class. Many students whipped out their phones to record the lesson, while others eagerly messaged friends or posted updates on the campus forums.

Before long, students from the second, third, and even fourth years began pouring into the lecture hall, ditching games, dates, and even skipping classes to attend.

"Johan-sensei?"

Down in the audience, Steven, Roxanne, and Flannery looked surprised to see him, but they quickly focused up, knowing that Johan's tactics were not to be missed, even if not directly adopted.

"Alright, you two, prepare your Pokémon," Duf announced, acting as the referee.

"Go!"

Kukui tossed a red-and-white Poké Ball, and an orange, hefty Pokémon appeared on the field.

"Oowu~~"

It was none other than a Dragonite, the pseudo-legendary Pokémon from the Kanto region!

In reality, Dragonite looked even rounder than one might imagine, especially with that chubby belly, which practically begged to be patted.

"A Dragonite?!"

"Come on, that's not fair, Royal Masked Man!"

"But Dragonite is so adorable~~"

Seeing Kukui's choice of Pokémon, Johan's expression turned a bit odd, but he released his own carefully chosen Pokémon.

"Woooo~~"

Appearing in front of Johan was a Lapras, with a tough shell on its back and a serene blue color.

Is this a preview of Lance versus Lorelei?

Or maybe a lesson for Lorelei on how to handle Lance? Johan thought to himself.

"It's not the Protect-Toxic strategy?"

"Lapras! Ice-type attacks are four times effective against Dragonite. This is in the bag for Johan-sensei!"

"Even I could do this!"

With Dragonite's four-time Ice weakness, strategy seemed almost unnecessary—just brute force with Ice-type moves would do.

Despite this, Kukui looked confident, and his confidence seemed to inspire Dragonite, who sharpened its gaze despite its plump appearance.

"Oowu! |ω)"

"Begin the battle!"

"Dragonite, Safeguard!" Kukui took the initiative.

Clearly, Kukui was worried about Johan's Protect-Toxic strategy, so he chose Dragonite, who could use Safeguard to avoid many potential problems. Even though Johan was using Lapras this time, Kukui opted for caution. After all, "Frozen" status could also be troublesome.

Seeing this, Johan smiled and gave a simple command:

"Lapras, Ice Beam."

The students were not wrong. When you have a four-time effectiveness, what else is there to do but charge ahead?

"Woooo—"

Lapras launched a pale blue, chilling beam from its mouth. But Kukui had anticipated this and grinned. He made a broad, sweeping motion with his hand.

"Dragonite, use Extreme Speed to dodge!"

In an instant, Dragonite's body was enveloped in a radiant white glow as it flapped its wings hard, dramatically increasing its speed.

Dragonite narrowly dodged the Ice Beam, only allowing its tail to graze the edge. The move Extreme Speed has a high priority, granting Dragonite a massive speed boost.

There are stories of Dragonite being able to circle the globe in 16 hours, most likely with Extreme Speed at its disposal.

If Dragonite and Kukui had the same level of synergy as Lance's Dragonite, perhaps it truly could dodge Ice Beam perfectly.

Plus, with Safeguard in effect, the frost that had grazed Dragonite's tail quickly melted away.

More importantly, Dragonite suddenly pulled out an apple with a bite taken out of it, rubbed it on itself, and took a hearty chomp.

It was holding the item Leftovers!

Dragonite regained a small amount of health.

Johan had always wondered, Whose leftovers are those? Why are they so effective?

"He really dodged it?!"

"Dragonite's way faster than Lapras!"

"Where's that guy who said 'even I could do this?'"

Students in the audience couldn't help but find the match increasingly interesting.

"Johan-sensei, type advantages don't determine everything," Kukui laughed confidently, his eyes sharpening.

"Now it's our turn!"

"Dragonite, use Thunder Punch!"

Johan sighed inwardly.

It seems we'll have to play like this after all.

"Lapras, sing your song—Perish Song!"

"Woooo~~"

A melodious, ethereal tune echoed from Lapras, like the gentle flow of a mountain stream. Dragonite's plump body visibly shuddered.

Kukui frowned.

In reality, Perish Song works by creating a special frequency of air vibrations that invoke fear deep within the target's mind. As the fear takes root and spreads, the affected Pokémon gradually loses its will to battle.

The battle had now turned into a race against time.

"Perish Song—is this a new strategy?"

"Isn't that a mutual defeat move?"

"…"

Zzzzzt—

Dragonite's Thunder Punch struck Lapras mid-song.

"Woooo!!"

Lapras let out a pained cry.

"Dragonite, one more time!" Kukui commanded.

Buzz—

But a faint green barrier enveloped Lapras.

Protect!

However, Dragonite's thunder-infused fist shattered the shield with a mighty punch.

Kukui's eyes flashed with determination.

Now's the moment!

"Outrage!"

Dragonite's eyes turned bloodshot as dark red energy swirled around it. It went berserk, slashing wildly at Lapras.

Kukui was exploiting the lowered success rate of using Protect consecutively, betting that Johan would follow the common "Protect-Don't Protect-Protect" tactic.

Although Outrage would leave Dragonite in a confused state afterward, it would also break through Lapras's Substitute, exploiting its speed advantage to land the final blow before Lapras could Protect again!

But Johan calmly instructed:

"Lapras, go to sleep."

Rest!

This Lapras had the Shell Armor ability, preventing critical hits.

And Johan equipped it with the item Focus Sash, ensuring it wouldn't be knocked out in one hit while at full health.

Using Rest, Lapras would completely restore its HP.

So even if Kukui had successfully exploited the gap between Protects, Lapras would still outlast him.

Because even with Perish Song affecting both, Dragonite's higher speed and larger, more aggressive movements would cause it to succumb first.

Moreover, Lapras also knew the move Sleep Talk, allowing it to successfully use Protect once more while asleep.

For a moment, the entire lecture hall was dead silent.

A few minutes later—

Dragonite's berserk rage from Outrage subsided, but its heart was now full of fear.

With a heavy thud, Dragonite plopped down, having lost its will to fight.

"Oowu…(ˇˇ)"

"The… the winner is Johan-sensei!"

Duf, a bit slow to react, finally announced the result.

And in an instant, the crowd erupted into cheers.

The Royal Masked Man had shown great skill, commanding Dragonite to dodge Ice Beam with Extreme Speed, shifting from a defensive to an offensive position. He had even successfully exploited the vulnerability in Protect.

But in the end, Johan's strategy was just… too resilient!

"That was so dirty! Hey, who's got a video of this? Send it to me!"

"I've got it! I need a battle partner right now—who's up for it?"

"Johan-sensei, what about in non-1v1 battles? How would this strategy work?"

After all, if a Pokémon hit by Perish Song was withdrawn, its effects would dissipate.

As Johan returned Lapras to its ball, he chuckled lightly.

"Lapras can learn Whirlpool or Block."

"Oh crap! Another counter?"

"I feel like I'm invincible now—every battle's gonna be a trade!"

"I hereby declare Johan-sensei as the Master of Tactical—no, Dirty Strategies!"

Johan stepped off the stage.

Gastly and Alolan Vulpix rushed up to him immediately.

"Oowu~~"

Alolan Vulpix's eyes were filled with admiration.

So this is what an Ice-type battle is like…

Johan picked up Alolan Vulpix and looked at Gastly. "Don't worry, this will also be one of your future strategic

reserves."

"KaKah!"

Gastly was about to cheer excitedly when its body suddenly shook.

Its tongue rolled out, bringing with it a phone covered in drool.

Johan accepted the phone with a twitch of his lips, seeing a friend request notification.

[King of the Land]: Please approve me.

He casually pressed "Reject."

"Good thing I set up a screening process. Who has the guts to call themselves the 'King of the Land?'"

While saying this, Johan subtly wiped his hand on Alolan Vulpix's fur.

Alolan Vulpix: "??"

# 59: A Fight Breaks Out

Kanto Region, Viridian City.

"Boss, why has Mewtwo gotten so much stronger?"

Sabrina pouted, her Kadabra by her side, looking displeased.

When it came to psychic abilities, Sabrina had never accepted defeat. Even though her Pokémon was still just a Kadabra and had not yet evolved into an Alakazam, its psychic strength could rival, if not surpass, many fully-evolved Alakazams.

More importantly, as a true psychic herself, Sabrina could amplify Kadabra's power using her own abilities, allowing it to unleash even greater destructive force.

That is, until she met Mewtwo. For the first time, Sabrina felt what it was like to be utterly overpowered in terms of psychic strength.

But she wasn't willing to accept it. She kept thinking about how she could use her abilities to defeat Mewtwo. This was also why she wasn't overly concerned about evolving Kadabra until recently, when she started wanting it to evolve.

Recently, an online Pokémon psychologist named Grain in the Field had inspired her, causing Kadabra's psychic powers—and even her own—to grow significantly.

Now she thought she could take on Mewtwo again.

But when she tried, Mewtwo's power had become even more monstrous.

"And that's not even all of Mewtwo's power," Giovanni said calmly, stroking his Persian.

Sabrina pursed her lips, knowing this was true. After all, Mewtwo was still wearing that armor designed to suppress its strength.

"Still, Sabrina, you've changed a lot recently."

Giovanni's gaze shifted to Kadabra by Sabrina's side.

As someone blessed by Viridian Forest and possessing the Power of Viridian, Giovanni could clearly sense the changes in Sabrina and Kadabra.

"Have I?" Sabrina looked at Kadabra.

Kadabra forced a grin.

"Kadabra—"

"Alright, I guess."

Sabrina shrugged. "Recently, I met a Pokémon psychologist online named 'Grain in the Field'."

"Oh?" Giovanni raised an eyebrow.

Sabrina stretched lazily, her silhouette revealing a smooth, graceful curve. "He seems pretty good at battle tactics too. Just beat Lance recently."

She yawned, waving her hand dismissively. "Boss, I'm going to bed. Kadabra and I have to cook later."

It's exhausting trying to compete with Mewtwo's psychic powers.

But the fact that sleeping and cooking could improve psychic powers… well, it was quite remarkable.

Cooking?

Giovanni's hand paused mid-stroke on Persian's fur, his brows furrowing.

A coincidence? Or…

"Sir, it's true. Sabrina does seem a bit different," said his secretary, Domino, softly.

"Domino, is the database restored?" Giovanni asked.

"N-no," Domino stammered slightly, trying to explain. "That… primarily, Porygon hasn't fully recovered from the Ka-kah attack, so…"

"Try reaching out to this 'Grain in the Field' person," Giovanni instructed.

Domino's pupils shrank. "Boss, are you saying…"

Could he be connected to Mewtwo?

Domino didn't press further, pulling out her phone to make the request. As Giovanni waited, Domino's fingers froze suddenly.

"B-Boss… I… I got rejected."

Giovanni: "???"

Hoenn Region, Rustboro Pokémon Academy.

"Ka-kah?"

Gastly tilted its head, confused, looking up at Johan.

"It's nothing. Just someone with a huge ego using an exaggerated nickname and failing to explain their issue. I declined."

Recently, there had been many like this, so Johan had set his phone to vibrate mode, only allowing notifications from actual patients.

Gastly glanced at the phone sneakily.

King of the Land?

"Ka-kah~~"

Woah, that's quite the bold nickname.

King of the Land? Where does that leave Groudon?

After Johan and Kukui stepped down, the stage was left to Duf once more.

In truth, the "Perish Song" strategy, though considered dirty by some, wasn't an unknown technique. Plenty of people were aware of it and even tried to use it; the problem was that few could use it effectively.

For instance, Agatha, who had given Johan the "Dread Manual," was a prime example of a Perish Song user.

However, Agatha's strategy was a far cry from Johan's. Her legions of ghost-type Pokémon were no joke, earning her the title of "The Warlord Queen" for a reason.

Similarly, in the future, Kanto's Ice-type master would also become a proficient user of the Perish Song strategy, serving as a significant roadblock for Lance on his path to the championship.

The thing about certain tactics is that knowing them is one thing, and executing them is another. Besides, strategies (read: dirty tricks) are flexible and varied. This isn't a game where each Pokémon can only have four moves; trying to copy strategies verbatim could lead to an embarrassing loss without understanding why.

Backstage—

"Johan-sensei, I didn't expect you to use the Perish Song strategy," Kukui said with a helpless smile.

Even with Dragonite's quadruple Ice-type weakness, Kukui wasn't worried. As a pseudo-legendary Pokémon, Dragonite's inherent strengths covered many weaknesses. However, the Perish Song strategy took him by surprise, as few could execute it well.

Johan chuckled softly.

He had considered other options. Kukui would definitely be on guard against something as simple as Substitute-Protect-Toxic.

Other moves like Spikes or Venoshock were not worth mentioning. Full defensive teams, sturdy Shedinja… these required more structure. Even something as solid as a Weakness Policy Sand Stream Palossand was a gamble.

As for Serene Grace Air Slash flinches… Johan questioned his own luck.

After all, he'd experienced five consecutive misses and seven straight failures before.

Plus, while the academy had many teaching Pokémon, he couldn't just pick any random one and expect its moves and abilities to fit.

Kukui was obviously strong, so the Perish Song strategy, which forced a one-for-one trade, was relatively reliable.

And besides, with Mega Evolution not yet widely available, something like the Mega-Glalie Explosion would have been ideal.

Hmm?

Wait a minute…

The thought suddenly struck Johan: if Lorelei knew about Mega-Glalie Explosions, Lance might never be able to overcome that hurdle in his life.

"Johan-sensei, I have a request," Kukui said seriously.

Johan was pulled from his thoughts.

"Hm? Please go ahead."

"If, and this is just an 'if,' the Alola region ever establishes a Pokémon academy, could we invite you to occasionally give a lecture?"

After a brief moment of hesitation, Johan nodded.

"If I have time, I wouldn't mind."

After all, he was planning to visit the Alola region eventually due to Alolan Vulpix. If his schedule allowed, he wouldn't mind helping out.

Plus, he was curious about that girl who once fished up a Feebas with a fishing rod.

"That's fantastic!" Kukui's face lit up with joy.

For someone dedicated to developing Alola, he greatly valued the opportunity to have someone like Johan on board.

"However, there's something I'd like to ask for help with in return, Royal Masked Man."

"Oh?" Kukui looked intrigued.

"Gastly, take Oowu out to play, but don't go too far."

Johan put Alolan Vulpix on the ground.

"Ka-kah!"

Gastly grinned mischievously, while Alolan Vulpix hesitated. With Johan's encouraging look, Vulpix finally followed Gastly away.

Though Kukui looked curious, he remained silent and waited for Johan to speak.

"Well, it's like this…"

After choosing his words carefully, Johan briefly explained Alolan Vulpix's situation from beginning to end.

After listening, Kukui frowned.

"Poaching? Or…"

In recent years, more and more people had been traveling to Alola, especially in the summer.

However, without a unified league structure in Alola, certain issues were challenging to address comprehensively, despite their best efforts.

If not for the island kahunas, who knows what kind of chaos might have ensued.

Taking a deep breath, Kukui said gravely, "I understand."

Even if it weren't for Johan and Alolan Vulpix, Kukui would still strive to investigate this matter now that he was aware of it.

Just then—

A student rushed into the room.

"Johan-sensei, your Gastly seems to be fighting with another Pokémon!"

"Hm?"

Meanwhile—

The video of Johan and Kukui's battle was spreading rapidly online.

# 60: Fighting for Milk?

Below Mt. Silver, Silver Academy, Kanto Region.

In a dorm room adorned with cute cartoons and overflowing with a girlish vibe, a tall girl with shiny black stockings and a tight black skirt, wearing thin-framed glasses, was hugging a chubby Jigglypuff plushie, her face lit with girlish delight.

Bam!!

Suddenly, the dorm door was flung open, and in walked another girl with long bluish-grey hair, wearing a yellow cropped top and strutting in golden high heels.

"Lorelei, you should—"

The girl holding the plushie was none other than the future Kanto Elite Four member and Ice-type master, Lorelei!

The bluish-grey-haired girl had just entered but stopped mid-sentence when she saw Lorelei's pale cheeks turn slightly pink as she hastily hid the Jigglypuff plushie behind her.

"Oh, stop hiding it, I saw it," the blue-haired girl teased playfully.

"Who would've thought that our 'cool ice queen' actually has such a girly side?"

Lorelei coughed lightly, her expression gradually returning to its usual cold demeanor.

"Lira, say what you came to say."

So, the bluish-grey-haired girl was Lira, the future Johto Elite Four Dark-type master!

Lira pouted in response.

"It's a shame no one else will see this side of you."

"Just check the League website. There's a 'Perish Song' strategy that's really interesting."

"Perish Song?" Lorelei raised an eyebrow as she reached for her tablet, wrapped in an Eevee-themed case.

"Not many people can use that strategy effectively. The only ones I know are Agatha—"

Opening the League website, she quickly found the video, which was rapidly gaining traction.

"Johan? This name… sounds vaguely familiar. And the opponent is the Royal Masked Man?"

The Royal Masked Man was more well-known than Johan, especially since his skills were recognized by many top trainers.

Curious, Lorelei started watching, her calm, icy eyes slowly revealing a look of surprise as the video progressed.

"Well?" Lira asked, crossing her legs and leaning on the table with a keen grin. "Pretty interesting, huh?"

Lorelei, engrossed in the video, didn't respond immediately.

After a while, she set the tablet down, her eyes flashing with contemplation as she murmured,

"Using 'Rest' with 'Focus Sash', alternating 'Substitute' with 'Protect', and opening with 'Perish Song'—if the strength gap isn't too large—"

"Tch, dirty, isn't it?" Lira said, teasing. "You use 'Perish Song' too, but this one's on another level."

Lorelei twirled a lock of hair between her fingers.

"It's got a bit of Agatha's touch to it, but it does fit Lapras quite well."

Lira leaned in, lowering her voice.

"Aren't you going to… take on that guy soon?"

Blackthorn City, Dragon's Den, Johto Region.

"Let's land, Dragonite."

Lance patted Dragonite, smiling.

Dragonite had just recently evolved, so Lance had been training in Blackthorn City these past few days, not just for Dragonite, but for Aerodactyl too.

"What is it, Clair?"

Clair, a girl with light blue hair tied in a ponytail, wearing a fitted outfit accentuating her figure, and the future Blackthorn City Gym Leader, handed Lance a tablet.

"Look, Johan's video is up."

"Really?"

Lance's eyes lit up as he took the tablet.

"Um… Big Bro, I'd advise not to—"

But Clair spoke too late. Lance and Dragonite were already watching the video intensely. They watched the entire thing in one go.

When they finished, Lance turned his head stiffly towards Dragonite.

"Rawr…?" Dragonite mumbled with a confused expression.

Hoenn Region, Rustboro Pokémon Academy.

Gastly getting into a fight with another Pokémon was the last thing Johan expected.

Typically, while mischievous, Gastly mostly liked to prank people within reason. It was surprising to see it actually engaging in a fight.

So, Johan immediately followed the student who reported the incident.

As per Johan's instructions, Gastly and Vulpix hadn't gone far. They were near a wooded area behind the lecture hall.

The Rustboro Academy's grounds were well-maintained with ample greenery, which meant not only students' Pokémon but also the Academy's Pokémon and even some wild ones roamed the campus.

The wild Pokémon on campus were usually mild-mannered and familiar with humans due to prolonged interaction with students and their Pokémon.

But there are always exceptions.

In the Rustboro Academy's small forest lived a group of Slakoth and Vigoroth.

They were known around campus as bullies, frequently teasing couples sneaking into the woods and pulling playful pranks on them.

It was rumored that these Slakoth and Vigoroth were left behind by a trainer who had once trained here.

Over time, students jokingly dubbed the area the "Couple's Burial Woods."

Or, more succinctly— "The Forest of Love's Demise."

When Johan arrived, he saw Gastly facing off against two Vigoroth.

Nearby, four or five Slakoth were sprawled out in deep sleep.

It was hard to tell whether they were naturally sleeping or if Gastly had put them under.

However, when Johan saw an empty MooMoo Milk bottle and Vulpix's teary eyes, he made a guess.

It seemed likely that Gastly had taken Vulpix for a stroll around the Academy, showing it the grounds while enjoying some MooMoo Milk. But then, a couple of Vigoroth must have appeared, trying to steal their milk, leading to this showdown.

A battle over milk?

A war for MooMoo Milk?

Come to think of it, Vigoroth, being a Normal-type with the "Vital Spirit" ability, did have an advantage over a Ghost-type like Gastly that specialized in control moves.

[Vital Spirit: Prevents the Pokémon from falling asleep.]

"Vigoooo!"

Before Johan could get closer, one of the Vigoroth growled and charged at Gastly with incredible speed.

"Gastly, Smog, then use Toxic!"

"Ka-kah!"

Upon hearing Johan's command, Gastly's eyes lit up with excitement.

It released a thick cloud of purple smog, obscuring the Vigoroth's vision, and then darted behind it. Sticking out its tongue mockingly, Gastly spat out a glob of sticky, venomous liquid onto the Vigoroth's back.

Hei~tui!

The second Vigoroth couldn't hold back any longer and lunged at Gastly.

"Vulpix, use Confusion!" Johan's footsteps gradually slowed.

"O-Oowu?"

Vulpix's wide eyes glanced between Johan, Gastly, and the approaching Vigoroth, unsure of what to do.

"You don't want to see Gastly get hurt, right?" Johan's voice was soft yet firm.

Hearing this, Vulpix grew increasingly anxious, whimpering softly, clearly hesitant and conflicted.

But as the Vigoroth's claws closed in on Gastly, Vulpix's expression shifted in an instant.

"Oowu!!"

A powerful psychic force erupted, sending both Vigoroth flying— the one hit with Toxic included.

"Ka-kah."

Watching the Vigoroth get flung away, Gastly reminisced about its own experience not long ago.

No, I definitely looked cooler when I got blasted away, it mused.

"What happened?"

Johan approached, frowning.

"Oowu~~"

Returning to its senses, Vulpix looked up at Johan with teary eyes, on the verge of crying.

I couldn't put them to sleep…

I couldn't put the Vigoroth to sleep… I'm not good enough, Vulpix seemed to be thinking.

Hmm?

Johan raised an eyebrow at Vulpix's explanation.

Perhaps the situation wasn't exactly as he had initially thought.

"Where are you going?"

Turning around, Johan caught Gastly trying to sneak away.

Gastly jolted, freezing in its tracks, and turned back with an awkward grin, tongue lolling out. It uncurled its tongue to reveal a Lum Berry, slightly marred by claw marks.

"Ka-kaah!"

Want some? It tastes pretty good.

Johan narrowed his eyes. "Is that all?"

"Ka-kah!"

That's it! I swear!

Not convinced, Johan grabbed Gastly, ignoring the tingling sensation in his fingers from the contact, and turned it upside down.

"Ka… ka… ka—"

From Gastly's mouth spilled five or six Lum Berries, three hairpins, two hats, two pens, and a couple of shed Vigoroth claws.

Johan's eyes narrowed further.

Are you a Gastly or a Greedent?

"Grrrrr!"

Suddenly, a deeper, more intimidating roar echoed from within the forest.
