# 61: A Knight’s Heart

In the Counseling Room

"Ka-kah~~"

Gastly floated by the wall, grinning awkwardly.

Despite its best efforts, it just couldn't manage to look serious.

Johan was sitting there, holding Alolan Vulpix in his arms. His hand gently ruffled through Vulpix's soft, cool, and fluffy fur while silently eyeing Gastly.

"Oowu~~"

Vulpix let out a purring sound, like a satisfied kitten, casting Gastly an apologetic look.

"So, what's the story?" Johan finally asked.

Just a little while ago, Gastly and Vulpix hadn't just put the already lazy Slakoth into a deep sleep estimated to last three days; they also drove away several Vigoroth.

They even managed to disturb the notorious Slaking, rumored not to have bathed in three years!

If Slaking had come out, Johan wouldn't have known how to handle the situation.

Now with the chance to explain, Gastly began to babble eagerly.

"Ka-kah ka-kah~~"

Listen to me! I have a totally reasonable explanation.

It turned out Gastly had taken Vulpix on a stroll around the area. They'd stumbled upon a couple of Vigoroth who were bullying two Academy Oddish by stealing their Lum Berries.

Would it let that slide? No way!

Gastly stepped in to knock down two of them but was then chased by more Vigoroth.

To make matters worse, the Vigoroth had also set their sights on the MooMoo Milk Gastly and Vulpix were enjoying. One of the Vigoroth even struck Vulpix, causing it to cry out, which inadvertently put several Slakoth into a deep sleep.

But could Gastly just sit back and let that happen? Absolutely not!

It relayed its tale with impassioned indignation, almost like a hero defending the weak.

After hearing it all, Johan pressed his lips together in thought before summarizing it all:

"So, basically… you went 'thief vs. thief'?"

"Ka-kah!!"

Gastly tried to protest further, but Johan had already made up his mind.

He sighed, rubbing his temples.

This is going downhill.

Breaking and entering.

Thief-on-thief action.

Gastly, you're getting deeper and deeper into the path of "Not-So-Legal Activities."

"As punishment, you're not playing on your phone tonight!" Johan said firmly.

"Ka-kah!! Σ(дlll)"

Gastly looked like it had turned to stone.

My Eevee! My Pikachu! My Popplio!

"And later, take some Pokéblocks to give to those Oddish," Johan added.

Gastly nodded dully, like its soul had left its body. Then, almost instinctively, it asked:

"Ka-kah…"

What about the Vigoroth?

Johan's eyes narrowed.

"Be glad we didn't escalate things with them."

Picking on Vulpix?

"Next time you're bored, go 'play' with them," Johan said with a hint of mischief in his voice.

"Ka-kaah…"

In the afternoon, Johan set up a training routine for Alolan Vulpix to practice controlling its psychic abilities.

Although there were some unexpected hiccups, at least nobody got seriously hurt.

Returning home that evening, Gastly quietly slipped into a corner, trying to sneakily take out its phone. But it could feel a gaze burning into its back.

"Ka-kah~~"

It grinned awkwardly, pretending the phone was meant for Vulpix to play with.

"Oowu?"

Vulpix looked puzzled.

Gastly stuffed the phone into Vulpix's paws.

"Ka-kah, ka-kah~~"

Tap here, tap there, that's right! Let's watch Popplio's Adventure together!

Alolan Vulpix tilted its head, utterly confused.

"Vulpix: ???"

Johan observed Gastly's "coaching" from the side but only chuckled, deciding not to intervene.

He had work of his own to handle.

The battle video had, without a doubt, been posted online, and a lot of people were trying to add him as a friend.

Some genuinely wanted to seek advice on their Pokémon's psychology, while others were simply curious or found it amusing.

Like that "King of the Land" from earlier.

So, Johan needed to sift through the requests, filtering out those who had legitimate questions from those just wasting time.

Even at this late hour, friend requests were trickling in. For instance:

[Bird on the Ground]: What should I do if my Pokémon and I aren't close?

That seemed like a reasonable question.

Johan approved the request.

[Grain in the Field]: Can you describe the situation?

The response came quickly.

[Bird on the Ground]: My Pokémon is really strong, super, super strong! But I have trouble… you know, connecting with them.

This seems like a typical case of a trainer having a powerful Pokémon that's beyond their control, Johan thought, somewhat reminiscent of a certain Charizard.

Speaking of which, is this world more akin to Red's or Ash's?

That was something Johan hadn't figured out yet.

He quickly refocused his mind.

Generally, there were two approaches to this problem:

A trainer could improve their skills and gain the Pokémon's respect through battles and victories.Alternatively, they could focus on strengthening the bond or friendship between them and their Pokémon.

Given the person's situation, Johan recommended the latter approach.

[Grain in the Field]: Are you able to communicate, or is communication itself an issue?

[Bird on the Ground]: We can communicate, but usually, he—uh, I mean, it—initiates most of our interactions.

Using voice-to-text, maybe? The typos are common.

But Johan didn't think too deeply about it. It couldn't possibly be another Mewtwo, right?

The odds were too low.

[Grain in the Field]: My suggestion is to focus on establishing a friendship or partnership first, and set aside the roles of trainer and Pokémon for the time being.

[Bird on the Ground]: How do I do that?

[Grain in the Field]: Whether it's a person or a Pokémon, winning their heart usually starts with winning their stomach.

Team Rocket Base, Viridian City, Kanto Region.

In a dimly lit room, Matori clutched a tablet, her face glowing softly in the eerie light. She looked almost giddy.

"Winning their stomach…"

She peeked over at Giovanni, who was working intently.

Sensing her gaze, Giovanni asked absentmindedly without looking up, "Did you connect?"

"Yes, sir!" Matori replied, stiffening up.

"Hm?" Giovanni paused, turning to face her.

"What did he say?"

Matori quickly dimmed the tablet's screen and answered, "I used an alternate account to add him. We're still in the initial stages of communication, and I can't confirm anything yet."

Giovanni nodded, shifting his focus back to his files.

"Start investigating his background."

"Understood!"

"His tactical skills are impressive; he might be worth recruiting."

"Yes, sir!"

Time passed.

Life continued with its usual rhythm of training challenges and moments of joy.

Alolan Vulpix gradually gained control over its abilities, while Gastly appeared to be growing stronger and more solid.

Sometimes Johan would don the guise of "Fivefold" and take his two Pokémon to the Battle Restaurant in Rustboro City to enjoy a hearty meal and gain some battle experience.

They began with the basic-level restaurants where novice trainers frequented and eventually felt confident enough to challenge intermediate and even advanced-level battle restaurants.

After all, even losing a battle just meant paying a bill that Johan had already planned for.

As a result, the name "Fivefold" started to become more recognized among the city's Battle Restaurants.

Even the initially wary restaurant owners came to realize the commercial value.

Fivefold drew a crowd, and by advertising his visits a day in advance, they could attract even more customers and aspiring trainers.

The urban legend of "Fivefold" continued to grow.

On this day, the Rustboro Pokémon Academy was celebrating its Fall Festival.

The campus was decked out in festive decor, exuding a lively and cheerful atmosphere.

Johan had donned a black suit, accented with golden trim at the cuffs and collar. He sported a monocle and carried a cane as he walked through the Academy grounds.

This suit was something he had splurged on—technically reimbursed—though he couldn't quite understand why the reimbursed attire had to be so lavish.

Gastly floated by, eagerly glancing around, its mouth stuffed with food, while Johan's wallet continued to grow lighter.

Walking by his side, Alolan Vulpix trotted along, wide-eyed with curiosity, as if experiencing everything for the first time.

Over time, Vulpix had become noticeably more sociable, no longer as shy as it once was.

"Professor Johan, you look so dashing today!"

"Wow, Alolan Vulpix is so adorable!"

"Need more food, Gastly? Want some more?"

Johan was

quite popular among the students these days.

"Professor Johan, would you like to pick out some outfits for your Pokémon?"

A female student dressed in a medieval evening gown called out from her shop, which sold Pokémon costumes.

Johan blinked, a little surprised.

"Oowu?"

Alolan Vulpix tilted its head, its eyes fixated on a Christmas-themed outfit inside the shop, its blue eyes sparkling with interest.

Gastly, on the other hand, stared blankly at a suit of knight's armor hanging on the wall, seemingly entranced.

Noticing their reactions, Johan chuckled.

"If you like something, go ahead and choose."

"But Gastly, I don't think the knight outfit is quite right for you… How about that mask instead?"

After all, Gastly only had a head—armor required at least a pair of arms.

"Ka-kah~~"

Gastly muttered, But I have a knight's heart…

Suddenly, Gastly's body began to glow with a brilliant white light!

Johan's expression froze.

Wait, was it really evolving just for a costume?

# 62: Evolution: Haunter!

"Oowu?"

Alolan Vulpix, Johan, and several students around them were all staring at Gastly, now engulfed in bright light.

"Ka-kah"

Even Gastly itself seemed a bit bewildered.

Within the shining glow, Gastly's form began to shift. Two arms gradually extended from its sides.

The gaseous mist surrounding its body started to solidify, forming an eerie, spiky face on either side.

Its once round, white eyes elongated into triangular slits, giving it a more sinister appearance. Black pupils now flickered with ghostly blue flames, and its previously small fangs turned into sharp, menacing ones.

As the light faded, Gastly's transformed shape fully emerged.

No longer Gastly… but now, Haunter!

"Ka-kah—"

Haunter looked at its newly formed hands, moving them about, fascinated by its own changes. Its voice had also grown deeper and more resonant than before.

"Haunter," Johan called out with a grin.

"Ka-kah?"

Haunter paused for a moment before realizing that Johan was addressing it. The newly evolved Pokémon happily narrowed its eyes and, sticking out its now larger tongue, darted toward Johan to give him a big, wet lick.

Johan's expression instantly shifted as he hurriedly shouted, "Wait! This outfit is expensive! The saliva… the saliva—!"

Slurp—

"Tsss— that paralysis is even stronger!"

He quickly bit into a Cheri Berry, its intense spiciness clearing the numbing sensation.

Johan shot a half-annoyed look at Haunter, who snickered mischievously. He had every reason to suspect Haunter was paying him back for his earlier comment about the knight armor not suiting it.

To be fair, Haunter's "Lick" was evidently much more potent post-evolution. Johan, who had started getting used to Gastly's Lick after repeated practice, now found himself starting from scratch—actually, worse than before.

"But regardless, congratulations on evolving, Haunter." Despite his playful resentment, Johan couldn't hide his genuine joy.

The feeling of seeing a Pokémon evolve after all the time and effort put in together was profoundly rewarding.

The transformation might have been triggered by an unexpected reason, but the evolution itself was undeniably a culmination of all their hard work.

"Ka-kah!"

Haunter blinked playfully, gesturing with its new hands.

(｀∀´)ノ

No biggie, piece of cake!

It then winked at Alolan Vulpix, who was watching it with wide eyes.

Haunter's eyes started glistening with tears, and it dramatically sniffled.

"Ka-kah~~"

It's been so hard, so very hard!

Finally, I can pet Vulpix too!

You have no idea how torturous it was to watch Johan pet Vulpix while I couldn't even touch it…

"Oowu~~"

Alolan Vulpix's eyes glowed faintly with psychic energy.

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter quickly waved its hands in surrender. Kidding! Just kidding! Don't take it so seriously!

As its gaze drifted toward the suit of knight's armor hanging on the wall, Johan couldn't help but twitch slightly.

So this really was about that armor…

A few minutes later.

Alolan Vulpix was dressed in a stylish navy-blue Christmas outfit, with the help of some female students who tied six light blue ribbons around its tails.

"Wow! So cute! Can I pet it?"

"My heart, I can't take it!"

"I'm dead! This is too much!"

The crowd that had initially gathered to watch Haunter's evolution was now swooning over the newly dressed Vulpix.

"It really does look great," Johan said, his eyes brightening with admiration.

"Oowu~~o(////▽////)q"

It's… it's not that great…

Bang, bang—

Suddenly, from the direction of the shop's fitting room, a figure clad in deep purple armor, with a helmet that emitted a faint glow, came stomping out.

When Johan saw this knightly figure emerge, he paused, muttering under his breath:

"Dark Inferno Knight?"

It wasn't an exact match, but… there really was some resemblance.

But the thing was… Haunter having arms made sense; it had claws, after all. But what was going on with those legs?

Curious, Johan approached and gently kicked the armored legs.

Clank, clank—

Empty?!

So the leg armor is just hanging there?

"Haunter?"

But something felt off to Johan.

Normally, Haunter should be smugly showing off and flaunting by now, right?

"Ka-kah"

I… I'm stuck… Help…

Johan sighed deeply.

I knew that big head of yours wouldn't fit inside a suit of armor!

In the end, Johan continued strolling around the festival grounds with Vulpix, now in its new Christmas outfit, and Haunter, floating in midair wearing only half a suit of armor.

Having hands had clearly made things easier for Haunter. It held an oversized ice cream in one hand, while the other hand held a spoon.

To be clear, the spoon was for Vulpix; Haunter preferred to chomp directly.

Before long, half the massive ice cream was gone.

The festival had plenty of attractions.

There were haunted houses, food stalls, pop-up shops, battle arenas, and more.

Haunter had wanted to explore the haunted house, but Johan quickly pulled it away, fearing that Haunter might accidentally scare the staff and ruin the experience for everyone else.

As they wandered, they eventually reached the battle zone.

This area was just as lively and decorated as the rest of the festival, attracting students and teachers alike who were passionate about Pokémon battles.

The festival's battle zone featured a variety of formats.

There were traditional 1v1s, 3v3 arena matches, double battles, tower challenges, and even special events.

Unlike other areas that were student-organized, the battle zone was managed by the Academy itself to prevent any potential issues.

For added safety, the Academy even brought in Nurse Joy from the Pokémon Center.

After all, Rustboro Academy was the largest Pokémon Academy in Hoenn, so they had enough clout to pull a few strings.

And since it was Academy-sponsored, there were naturally prizes to motivate participants.

"Good evening, Nurse Joy."

Having met her before, Johan greeted Nurse Joy at the medical station.

"Professor Johan." Nurse Joy's eyes lit up, and she covered her mouth with a chuckle, "You look very handsome tonight."

"Thank you."

"Ka-kah!" Haunter saluted with its new claws, waving to Nurse Joy and her Chansey.

"Haunter? Did it evolve?" Nurse Joy observed.

Johan felt a bit awkward.

At least she didn't ask how it evolved… That would be too embarrassing to explain.

"Oowu~~"

The timid Vulpix also gave Nurse Joy a soft greeting, showing its progress.

"Aww, Vulpix, you're so adorable!"

"Lukky~~"

"Are you planning to participate in the tournament, Professor Johan? I heard the prizes this year are quite good," Nurse Joy asked.

"Prizes…"

"Professor Johan!"

Just then, Steven, dressed in his signature suit, approached them with a bright smile.

"I was looking for you earlier, but I just knew I'd find you here."

What do you mean 'just knew'? Johan thought.

"Haunter? Your Gastly evolved?" Steven noticed Haunter's armor, chuckling as he continued, "When did this happen?"

While speaking, he also greeted Haunter and Vulpix warmly.

Johan sighed inwardly.

Ahem, let's change the topic. "Steven, did you enter the tournament yet?"

"No, not yet." Steven shook his head, smiling. "But I know the Academy put a lot of effort into this. The final prize for the tower challenge is a set of Elemental Gems."

"Ka-kah?"

Hearing this, Haunter's eyes gleamed with interest.

# 63: Haunter’s Hunting Hour!

"Do you want that set of Elemental Gems?"

Seeing Haunter's excitement, Johan asked with a smile.

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter scratched its head, the armor on its body making clinking noises as it shifted. The gems were secondary; Haunter was simply eager to battle and test its new abilities after evolving.

Well then.

Haunter had become clever—it was hard for Johan to discern its true motives. Or maybe, Haunter wanted both the battle and the reward.

However…

Johan also wanted to gauge Haunter's strength.

It's said that Haunter is a dimensional creature, capable of slipping into other dimensions. Some trainers who owned Haunters often had them hide in their shadows as a form of protection. Agatha, one of the Elite Four, was particularly skilled in this technique. It's rumored that not even those close to her knew how many Ghost-type Pokémon were hiding in her shadow.

In this world of Pokémon, a trainer must always be vigilant. When facing opponents who specialize in wild, no-holds-barred battles or those skilled in ambush tactics, even the Elite Four could be caught off guard.

From Johan's recollection, the most striking example of this was Gold from Pokémon Adventures. In normal battles, Gold often struggled, but when it came to no-holds-barred situations, his precision and stealth were terrifying. Then, of course, there's Agatha and Lance—though Lance is more of a cautionary tale.

Now that Gastly had evolved into Haunter, it was like having an extra layer of protection for Johan.

"Wanna give it a shot?"

"Ka-kah!!"

Haunter let out a manic laugh, twirling its long tongue in excitement.

It was hunting time!

Hearing this, Steven's eyes lit up.

Normally, he wasn't too interested in participating in school festival battles. But if Johan was joining, he couldn't help but get intrigued.

However, to Steven's surprise, Johan didn't head towards the 1V1 or 2V2 arenas.

Instead, he walked straight toward the "Tower Climb Zone," which had fewer people around.

The "Tower Climb Zone" is self-explanatory: challengers had to progress through a series of increasingly difficult opponents, like climbing a tower. There were no breaks, and no healing items could be used in between battles. The higher a challenger climbed, the more rewards they earned at each stage.

More importantly, with each stage, the opposing Pokémon would be slightly stronger than the previous ones. It was a test of both the trainer's strategy and their Pokémon's capabilities.

Since the event was organized by the Academy, the opposing Pokémon were carefully selected to match the challenger's Pokémon strength. The early rounds wouldn't just consist of weak opponents.

"Ka-kah?"

Haunter looked around at the relatively quiet area, puzzled, and turned to Johan for an explanation.

Here?

Holding Vulpix in his arms, Johan offered a simple reason:

"As a teacher, battling students wouldn't look good. People might think we're bullying them—especially some of those big baby-like opponents."

Steven: "??"

Why did it sound like Johan was calling out specific people?

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter nodded with understanding, having developed a good rapport with Johan.

One thing was clear:

Steven had his eye on them, so they were keeping a low profile.

Before challenging, Johan needed to confirm Haunter's new moves after evolving. At Johan's direction, Haunter's eyes glowed faintly.

In Johan's line of sight, Haunter's updated Pokédex entry appeared.

While not many changes were immediately visible, Haunter had gained the move "Shadow Punch."

Of course, it was a Physical move, and Haunter excelled in Special Attack, so "Shadow Punch" was mostly negligible.

Still, the full extent of Haunter's evolution needed to be explored through further training and real battles.

"Professor Johan, climbing the tower?"

The bored-looking advisor of Class 2, Year 1 of the Battle Department, Isaya, perked up with interest.

She had previously worked in the Battle Frontier's Battle Factory, making her well-versed in various Pokémon and their capabilities. She was perfect for overseeing the Tower Climb Zone.

The two weren't close friends, but they were familiar enough as fellow faculty members to exchange more than a nod in passing.

"Professor Isaya."

Johan greeted her with a friendly nod.

"Don't expect me to hold back," Isaya cautioned.

"Of course." Johan agreed easily. "I just want to test Haunter's strength. It recently evolved."

Isaya seemed to understand.

She clapped her hands on a button set in the wall of the Tower Climb Zone.

Boom!

A large screen on the wall of the zone lit up, displaying a giant zero.

The noise caught the attention of a few nearby students.

Originally, they weren't very interested, but upon seeing Johan step up to the challenge, they became curious.

Simultaneously, Isaya held a Pokéball in her hand.

Seeing that Haunter had only just evolved…

"Then the first opponent will be Beedrill!"

In a flash of red light, a Beedrill with sharp, needle-like forelimbs appeared on the field.

"Haunter," Johan called calmly.

With its typing advantage, Haunter was in a good position.

"Ka-kah!!"

Haunter shed its knight armor and set it aside, grinning wildly with excitement.

Finally, my time to shine!

(｀<´)

Bring it on!

"Beedrill, Agility!" Isaya commanded, waving her hand decisively.

Buzz, buzz, buzz—

Beedrill's wings vibrated rapidly, its form flickering as it moved at high speeds, appearing at various corners of the battlefield.

But neither Johan nor Haunter moved. Haunter's eyes kept following Beedrill's movements, trying to pinpoint its position.

At the same time, Johan's line of sight revealed Beedrill's full Pokédex entry.

The only Dark-type move it possessed was "Payback."

It had a fair amount of Bug- and Poison-type moves, but Haunter, being part-Poison itself, didn't need to worry about the Poison-type attacks. As for Bug-type moves, Haunter quadruple-resisted them.

Seeing Haunter's lack of movement, Isaya frowned.

It made things difficult for her.

"Beedrill, Focus Energy!"

"Hypnosis!"

Just as Isaya's command to focus was given and Beedrill stopped moving, Johan's order cut through the air.

"Ka-kah!"

Haunter let out a sharp cry.

This caught Beedrill's attention for a split second.

And that was all Haunter needed.

Beedrill's once-intense gaze softened, becoming foggy, and then drowsy.

"Haunter, Hex."

Haunter's eyes turned blood-red, the crimson glow reflecting within Beedrill's mind.

Boom!!

An ominous, muffled explosion echoed from within Beedrill. It fell to the ground, fainting on the spot!

"Ka-kah! (｀д′)"

Next!

Boom!

The screen displayed "1" as the number zero turned into one.

Isaya recalled Beedrill, casting a contemplative look at both Haunter and Johan.

Just a single "Hypnosis" followed by "Hex" had taken down her Beedrill.

While Beedrill was naturally a bit frail, this still indicated that Haunter's power wasn't to be underestimated.

"In that case, the second opponent will be Kingler!"

A Kingler with massive pincers materialized on the battlefield.

"Kingler, use Bubble Beam!"

A Kingler with the "Sheer Force" ability?

The Academy certainly had a wide variety of Pokémon.

But Johan's strategy this time was entirely different, so when Isaya gave her command, he already had his instructions in place.

"Haunter, hide in its shadow."

As Kingler spat out bubbles, Haunter had already reappeared behind it!

At close range, Haunter unleashed a "Clear Smog," enveloping Kingler in toxic fumes. It had no chance to escape.

A sickly purple glow spread across Kingler's body, indicating its poisoned state.

"Finish it with Hex."

The same move to close the battle once more.

It wasn't that Johan didn't want to use other moves. It's just that with Haunter mastering paralysis, poison, confusion, sleep, and flinch-inducing techniques, there wasn't a move more suitable than "Hex."

"Ka-kah!"

Haunter stood triumphantly atop the unconscious Kingler, sticking out its tongue.

Do you know the power of an ambush from behind?

Boom!!

The number on the screen jumped from "1" to "2."

The repeated noise drew even more attention.

"Ka-kah!"

I'll take on ten of them!

# 64: Who Else Wants Some!

"Steven, you're here too?"

Around the "Tower Climb Zone," more and more people gathered. Initially, they were merely onlookers, but as time passed, their reactions shifted from casual curiosity to shock, then to stunned silence. Roxanne, dressed in a maid outfit, and Flannery, in a hip-hop outfit, squeezed through the crowd and found Steven standing at the front.

"What's… happening here?" Flannery asked, looking around.

Her gaze swept over the scene, taking note of the surprised expressions on the faces around her and the change of the number on the wall from "7" to "8." Finally, she focused on Johan and Haunter in the center of the stage.

"Professor Johan's Gastly… evolved?" Roxanne said in surprise.

With his arms crossed and his chin resting on his fingers, Steven smiled slightly.

He wasn't all that surprised.

In his understanding, Professor Johan's tactical prowess was unmatched. Neither Lance nor the Masked Royal could match Johan's abilities.

And "tower climbing" was an event that truly tested a trainer's strategic thinking—judging the situation, managing the Pokémon's status, and achieving the most with the least effort. This suited Johan perfectly.

Moreover, Haunter's evolution meant its strength had significantly increased.

Steven's lack of surprise didn't mean others shared his sentiment.

"Eight… Professor Johan has taken out eight in a row?!" Flannery blurted out.

To be more precise, Johan and Haunter had consecutively defeated eight Pokémon together!

Granted, these eight Pokémon were all part of the Academy's training team and not perfectly synchronized with Isaya's commands, but they were far from weak. Isaya herself was known for being adept at leading Pokémon battles using random choices.

On the battlefield, Johan looked at Haunter, who was laughing triumphantly, and asked:

"Haunter, how are you holding up?"

"Oowu?"

The Alolan Vulpix in Johan's arms also glanced over with concern.

"Ka-kah! (〝▼皿▼)"

Haunter licked its claws with confidence.

This is my hunting hour. Ready or not, I'll keep going!

Haunter's stamina seemed fine, and Johan could see from his visual interface that Haunter's overall condition wasn't bad.

What Johan was really asking was whether Haunter had had enough fun.

But clearly, Haunter wasn't satisfied yet.

However, the Pokémon Isaya sent out were getting stronger with each round.

"Ka-kah!" Bring on the next one!

"Ninth Pokémon," Isaya called out, holding a Poké Ball.

Despite being on the losing end, Isaya showed no signs of frustration. As the overseer of the "Tower Climb Zone," she understood the importance of keeping her cool to ensure fairness.

To be honest, she was also curious about how far Johan and Haunter could go.

She could sense that while Haunter hadn't sustained any major injuries, the continuous use of moves and abilities had to be wearing on its stamina.

Well, sort of…

Haunter occasionally pulled a "Toxic Orb" from its mouth and licked it like a snack, as if tricking itself into feeling better.

"Go, Hypno!"

The next Pokémon that emerged from the flash of red light was a humanoid Pokémon with golden fur, a white ruff around its neck, and a small pendulum in its hand.

"Suri."

A Psychic-type and a master of hypnosis—Hypno!

Upon entering the field, Hypno's eyes glowed with a deep blue hue, and it locked its gaze onto Haunter.

"Ka-kah!"

Haunter immediately folded its arms defensively and shivered in horror.

"Professor, there's a perv on the loose!" it seemed to say.

It felt like it was being completely seen through.

"'Forewarn' ability, huh?" Johan narrowed his eyes. "So, which move will you predict?"

Normally, "Forewarn" identifies the move with the highest base power. But in reality, it likely focuses on the move most threatening to Hypno.

At this moment, Hypno was confused.

The move it identified as the greatest threat was… Lick?!

Isaya was momentarily taken aback.

However, the battle couldn't pause for surprises. After quickly recovering her composure, Isaya called out:

"Hypno, use Hypnosis!"

"Suri."

Hypno's narrow eyes glinted slyly as it gently swayed its pendulum, sending ripples of hypnotic energy outward.

"Haunter, Hypnosis," Johan commanded in a calm tone.

Haunter let out a sinister chuckle and waved its hands as its eyes began to glow.

This was the secret code between Johan and Haunter.

When Johan said "use Hypnosis," Haunter would use the traditional move, Hypnosis.

But when Johan said just "Hypnosis"…

Ka-kah ka-kah ka-kah…

"Challenging a 'Hypnosis Specialist' at their own game?" Many spectators were baffled by Johan's choice.

Only Steven, who had witnessed Johan and Haunter's hypnotic prowess, kept a serious expression, waiting for the outcome.

Isaya didn't understand Johan's reasoning, but soon, she sensed something unusual.

Huh?

Under her astonished gaze, Hypno's body wobbled slightly.

Thud!

It collapsed to the ground!

"Whoa—"

The crowd collectively gasped, expressions of disbelief on their faces.

What just happened?!

A Hypno, a Pokémon specialized in Hypnosis, had been outmatched in its own domain by Haunter!

If they hadn't seen Hypno fall with their own eyes and witnessed its body twitching, no one would have believed what they saw.

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter yawned and patted its mouth, a tear forming at the corner of its eye.

Whew, that was close. I almost fell asleep myself.

Then, Haunter smirked mischievously as it surveyed Hypno's body, its gaze finally landing on the hypnotic pendulum.

"Ka-kah~~"

"Oowu."

The Alolan Vulpix's eyes sparkled.

Is this… the fabled 'Hypnosis Technique' of the House of Johan?

"What just happened, Professor Johan?" someone in the audience couldn't hold back their curiosity and asked.

Johan gave a straight-faced, confident explanation: "Hypno's Hypnosis is a wide-area effect, while Haunter's is a direct, focused one."

Everyone nodded in understanding…

Not!

Boom!

The number on the wall changed from "8" to "9"!

Nine down!

Isaya took a deep breath, recalling Hypno and preparing her next Pokémon.

"Then… the tenth one."

A flash of red light was accompanied by a rumbling vibration in the ground.

The large, blue-scaled Gyarados stood tall, its eyes wide with fury as it unleashed an ear-splitting roar!

"Roooar—!!"

The Water/Flying-type Pokémon—Gyarados!

Johan frowned slightly.

In front of this massive creature, Haunter seemed quite small.

"Ka-kah—!! (〝▼皿▼)"

What are you roaring for? You startled me!

Haunter slowly sank into the shadows.

Facing such a behemoth head-on would be foolish.

"Gyarados, use Dragon Dance!" Isaya commanded, initiating with "Dragon Dance."

"Rooooar!"

Gyarados reared back, roaring as its power surged, whipping up small whirlwinds around the field with incredible speed.

In such a whirlwind of chaos, even approaching Gyarados would be difficult.

"Toxic!"

Haunter sneaked up behind the twirling Gyarados and spewed out a glob of sticky purple sludge.

"Here it comes, Professor Johan's 'Sub-Protect-Toxic' strategy!"

"Gyarados, use Aqua Tail and flush Haunter out of the shadows!"

Crash—

Water swirled around Gyarados's tail like a mighty whip as it smashed down at the ground where Haunter's shadow lay.

Boom!!

The ground exploded, water splashing everywhere, and Haunter was forced out, shattering a green protective barrier around it.

She must be holding onto 'Outrage' to counter 'Hypnosis'… Johan thought.

"Crunch!"

Seeing Haunter revealed, Gyarados roared menacingly, slamming its tail down and charging at Haunter with terrifying speed.

Boom!

Its jaws clamped down on an illusion—a Substitute.

Isaya's eyes flashed.

"Dragon Dance!"

She had predicted the standard pattern of the "Sub-Protect-Toxic" strategy—protect, don't protect, then protect. But…

Johan had anticipated her prediction.

"Haunter, Hex!"

Sometimes, strategies as simple as "Sub-Protect-Toxic" required flexibility. Following a rigid routine would be akin to throwing the match.

It had to be acknowledged that being able to see the opposing Pokémon's full move list and its proficiency level gave Johan a significant advantage.

This final move solidified the victory.

A few minutes later…

Boom!

Gyarados's massive form wobbled, then collapsed heavily to the ground.

Boom!!

The number on the wall changed from "9" to "10"!

Haunter pulled out a pair of sunglasses from nowhere and put them on.

It raised its head proudly and scanned the crowd.

"Ka-kah!! |ω)"

Who else wants some!!

Haunter had completed a streak of ten!

# 65: A “New” Patient

"Continue?" Isaya glanced at Haunter, who was brimming with excitement, then turned her gaze to Johan.

The Academy's "tower climbing" record was twelve levels.

However, in recent years, no one had been able to reach or surpass the tenth level.

After all, the strength of the Pokémon sent out was adjusted according to the challengers' Pokémon, and the difficulty steadily increased. Achieving a high level wasn't something that could be done by simply bulldozing through with brute force.

Moreover, the stronger the challenger's Pokémon, the harder it was to climb higher.

"Ka-kah!"

Haunter removed its sunglasses and nodded vigorously.

Of course, keep going!

It wore an expression of "I'm feeling super brave!"

"Not today," Johan replied with a smile, declining the offer.

"Ka-kah?"

Haunter looked at Johan, confused.

What about our bond of trust?!

Johan glanced at Haunter with a hint of amusement.

"Weren't you just saying you wanted to eat a whole Tauros? Or would you prefer to head over to the Pokémon Center for dinner instead?"

Oh right!

Haunter quickly came to its senses, and in a flash, it zipped back to Johan's side and gave him a solid lick with its tongue.

"Ka-kah—"

Johan still cares about me.

Wait, where's my unfinished ice cream?

Isaya and the surrounding students showed a bit of disappointment.

They had hoped to witness Johan and Haunter create a new record.

But Johan knew that breaking the twelve-level record would be tough for Haunter in its current state.

While Haunter seemed to breeze through earlier battles, that Hypno, if it had fought under normal conditions, would have given Haunter a significant challenge.

And the Gyarados was an even greater threat, forcing Haunter to use its ace strategy: Sub-Protect-Toxic.

Although Haunter didn't get physically hit, the move Substitute was a massive drain on its energy.

Plus, Haunter currently lacked any self-recovery moves.

With future opponents bound to grow even stronger, continuing the climb might lead to injuries.

They had already earned the highest reward, so there was no need to push further.

If Haunter could master a move like Dream Eater to sustain itself, it might have been a different story.

In any case, quick-moving students had already uploaded videos of Johan and Haunter's "Ten-Win Streak" to the campus forum.

The videos stirred up quite the buzz.

Within the walls of Rustboro Academy, an emerging fanbase had even formed without Johan realizing it—a group called the Grain Fan Club!

"Professor Johan, here's your prize." Isaya handed over a box with a smile. Inside was a set of elemental gems.

"Thank you."

"Ka-kah~~ ()"

Pokémon Center

Haunter squinted contentedly while receiving a quick recovery treatment from Nurse Joy and her Chansey. Its expression was cheeky enough to make both Nurse Joy and Chansey giggle.

Steven and the others didn't follow along this time.

Having witnessed Johan and Haunter's impressive "streak," they were inspired to dive back into their own battles, ready to "beat up some rookies."

Johan felt a sense of relief seeing that Steven had been quite busy lately, presumably training hard or preparing for some competition.

Opening the prize box, Johan found the elemental gems arranged neatly inside, with only the Dragon Gem and Fairy Gem missing.

He casually picked up a Fire Gem.

Too bad it's not a Fire Stone, but it should still be worth quite a bit, right? he thought.

If the reward had been a set of Evolution Stones, the Academy would have probably gone broke.

After all, each Evolution Stone costs millions, and a full set could likely feed Haunter for a long time.

Wait a minute…

Johan's thoughts came to a halt.

Haunter had evolved, which meant its appetite and dietary quality requirements were bound to increase.

A wave of pressure washed over Johan once more.

He carefully put the gems away and rubbed his temples.

I really need to keep earning money to support these two gluttons.

Seems like I'll have to speed up my progress on learning how to make Pokéblocks.

In truth, Johan had been learning how to make them for some time, with Daisy's guidance, but he still didn't have a Berry Blender of his own. Constantly borrowing the school's equipment for practice wasn't a sustainable solution.

And Berry Blenders weren't exactly cheap—definitely pricier than a coffee machine.

If only there were more wealthy clients like that 'Eevee Eats Cabbage' lady…

Johan sighed inwardly.

"Professor Johan, it's about time."

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter got up and stretched, clearly enjoying the massage.

It hadn't been injured, so all it needed was a simple recovery session, which didn't take long at all.

Thanking Nurse Joy, Johan resumed exploring the Academy grounds with Haunter and Alolan Vulpix.

Haunter put its armor back on, and after showing off with Alolan Vulpix over their giant ice cream sundae, it all ended with…

Click—

Johan took a picture and uploaded it to his online profile, captioning it, "Autumn Ice Treats."

As Johan gained some notoriety online, he occasionally posted about daily life or shared tips about Pokémon.

However, he rarely replied to comments, as he was swamped with questions from the online psychological consultation room.

Later…

Johan took Haunter and Alolan Vulpix out for Tauros BBQ.

Then, they enjoyed a Kantonian-Style massage at the Machamp Massage Parlor.

They stopped at the Smeargle Photography Studio for some commemorative photos.

Lastly, they watched an Eevee performance at the "Eevee Performance Club," wrapping up the busy evening.

Overall, this Academy Festival had been a lot of fun for them.

When they got home…

Haunter skillfully pulled out its phone and began watching the still-ongoing Bibarel Adventures series with Alolan Vulpix.

Johan was pretty sure this show had no end in sight. A series with each episode being a standalone story? How could it possibly conclude?

Meanwhile, Johan dealt with the soreness in his back, still bruised from the Machamp massage.

He added nutrients to the Pokémon Egg incubator while browsing the latest news.

Something interesting caught his eye.

"Lance VS Lorelei, Dragons vs. Ice Showdown!"

The headline detailed the showdown between Lance and Lorelei, even mentioning a Lapras vs. Dragonite battle.

The result ended with a forced one-for-one between the two Pokémon, almost a mirror of Johan's earlier battle with Professor Kukui.

"Man, Lance's 'Blaze' Charizard is kinda broken—he can even one-shot a Thick Fat Dewgong…"

As he was reading, a friend request notification popped up on his phone.

[Forever Eighteen]: What should I do if my Pokémon is too self-absorbed?

Forever Eighteen? Johan raised an eyebrow.

Anyone using a name like "Forever Eighteen" was almost certainly past that age—that was Johan's "life experience."

However, the person's question did seem related to Pokémon psychology, so he accepted the request.

[Grain in the Field]: Could you describe the details? After all, confidence isn't necessarily a bad thing.

[Forever Eighteen]: What if they feel like they'll die if they don't look in the mirror every day… that kind of 'confidence'?

Uh…

Johan ran his hand over his forehead.

That felt like a personal attack.

The person then sent a photo.

It was a Smoochum staring at itself in a mirror, carefully applying lipstick.

# 66: Vulpix’s “Rival”

Smoochum, the Kiss Pokémon, is of the Ice and Psychic types.

It resembles a small girl with golden hair, pink skin, large eyes, and its signature thick lips.

Smoochum is naturally curious, and its lips are very sensitive. When it encounters something unfamiliar, it often uses its lips to touch and understand it first.

Its lips are also used to remember things it likes and dislikes.

To keep its lips moisturized, wild Smoochum regularly use tree sap as a lip balm.

It's not surprising for a Smoochum to constantly check its lips, even after taking a fall. So, its fixation with mirrors isn't unusual.

But if it truly feels like it would "die" without a mirror, that's no longer confidence—it's vanity.

This wasn't strictly a psychological issue.

It's somewhat similar to Eevee Eats Cabbage's Feebas. But while that Feebas was excessively self-conscious, this "[Forever Eighteen]" Smoochum is excessively vain.

After giving it some thought, Johan offered his advice.

[Grain in The Field]: "My suggestion is to intervene externally while also redirecting its focus, coupled with some 'hypnotic' guidance."

[Forever Eighteen]: "What specific method?"

[Grain in The Field]: "For example, you could hide all the mirrors. Or, you could try taking away its mirror while it's using it and offer it something like a Pokéblock to distract it."

[Forever Eighteen]: "One moment."

It wasn't long before the person sent over a video clip.

In the video, Smoochum was happily looking in the mirror, when suddenly, a pale hand snatched it away. In the next instant, Smoochum burst into tears.

The tears formed a small puddle on the ground, and Smoochum simply used the puddle as a mirror instead.

As for the offered Pokéblock, it didn't even spare a glance at it.

Johan: "…"

He retracted his earlier judgment.

This was indeed a psychological issue!

[Forever Eighteen]: "So… that's the situation."

Johan felt a slight headache coming on.

If a Pokémon was overly self-conscious, it could be helped by steadily boosting its confidence.

But if a Pokémon was overly confident to the point of vanity, it couldn't be cured with a heavy-handed approach.

Otherwise, it could backfire and lead to the opposite extreme—causing the Pokémon to spiral into deep self-doubt, which would be much harder to fix.

Otherwise, simply showing Smoochum what it would look like after evolution could suffice.

There was only one solution left.

Johan sighed.

He glanced at Alolan Vulpix, who was happily watching TV alongside Haunter.

"Well, you probably won't meet it anyway," he thought.

He then sent over a picture of Alolan Vulpix sitting in an ice cream cup from the recent Academy Festival.

[Forever Eighteen]: "So cute!"

"Message retracted."

Johan raised an eyebrow and pretended not to see that.

[Grain in The Field]: "Show it this, and let me know its reaction."

Soon after, a new photo arrived.

Smoochum was frozen in place, holding the tablet with a stunned expression.

Johan let out a sigh of relief.

Thankfully, Alolan Vulpix's cuteness worked like a charm.

[Forever Eighteen]: "It looks… a bit lost."

[Grain in The Field]: "Are you a Trainer?"

[Forever Eighteen]: "Yes."

[Grain in The Field]: "Then tell it that Alolan Vulpix trains hard, and that a Pokémon who trains is the most beautiful."

[Forever Eighteen]: "I'll try."

Ding-ding!

Another picture arrived. This time, Smoochum was earnestly lifting a pair of tiny dumbbells.

It seemed to be working, at least a little.

[Grain in The Field]: "This is just the first step. Keep a close eye on Smoochum's condition, and remember to praise it with words like 'strong,' 'diligent,' and 'hardworking' are what make a Pokémon truly beautiful. If there's any more trouble, feel free to reach out."

Confidence is a good thing, but if Smoochum becomes too fixated on its appearance, it could lead to a significant drop in self-esteem once it evolves.

Of course, it's possible that even after evolving, it could remain confident in its appearance—but in a more delusional way.

That's why it was necessary to shift its focus early on.

Johan specifically asked if they were a Trainer to gauge the best direction for this shift.

If they only saw Smoochum as a pet, the focus wouldn't have been as effective.

[Forever Eighteen]: "Thank you! (sigh of relief emoji)"

[Grain in The Field]: "You're welcome."

After that, Johan answered a few more messages.

He set the incubator back in place, took a quick shower, and prepared to go to bed with Alolan Vulpix.

Lately, he hadn't needed to "help with sleep" as much.

Well, it's not like I can freeload every day…

Silph Academy, Johto

"Heh, little Smoochum working out? Is the sun rising in the west tomorrow?"

Karen pushed open the door and found Smoochum diligently lifting its small dumbbells, making little grunting noises. She looked utterly surprised.

Lorelei adjusted her glasses and explained what she had consulted Johan about online earlier.

"Oh? Seems like our Senior Johan really knows his stuff."

They had felt a sense of familiarity with Johan, only to later discover that he had also attended Silph Academy, even though they weren't in the same department. Technically, he was their senior.

"Karen, how do you think I should thank him?" Lorelei asked.

Karen understood this wasn't just about the Smoochum matter. Lorelei had also gained insights from the Perish Song strategy.

"Just pay him, I guess."

Karen shrugged.

"I heard our Senior Johan used to work multiple part-time jobs."

Lorelei frowned.

It wasn't that she thought paying money was crude.

She was just worried that giving him money might hurt his pride.

"You're overthinking things," Karen said, rolling her eyes.

"Then, give him some training insights. He has an Alolan Vulpix, doesn't he? Since you're so good with Ice-types, give him some advice on raising them as a token of thanks."

"You think a Trainer who can command a Lapras to use Perish Song so adeptly needs my Ice-type insights?" Lorelei countered.

"True." Karen's eyes lit up mischievously. "Didn't Lance meet him too? You could always ask Lance…"

CRACK!

Lorelei's tablet suddenly displayed a noticeable crack.

After the Festival, the Academy granted a short holiday break.

Early in the morning, poverty drove Johan to wake up bright and early.

Upon waking, he saw Haunter on the ceiling, rubbing its sleepy eyes and looking disappointed.

Another failed attempt to lick Johan awake…

"Oowu—"

Alolan Vulpix stretched lazily, front and back legs extending in a long, luxurious stretch.

Today, Johan planned to head to the electronics district, hoping to find a second-hand Berry Blender. He had already prepared a detailed guide for the outing.

However, not long after getting out of bed, he heard a voice from downstairs.

"Hello, delivery for you! Please sign here!"

By the window, a Pidgeotto was preening its feathers while clutching a package.

A delivery?

Johan was puzzled.

# 67: Trickery and the Invitation

Package?

"Ka-kah—"

Haunter immediately floated over to the window, cupping its hands around its mouth like a megaphone and shouting at the delivery person below, almost giving the poor deliveryman a heart attack.

With Pidgeotto watching nervously, the package was handed over.

When Johan saw the sender's name, he smiled.

Lance.

He recalled that last time, when Drake sent a package, it arrived via Dragonite. But this time, Lance's package came through a Pidgeotto, even though it belonged to the delivery person. Somehow, Johan felt like this was a subtle omen of something.

"Ka-kah~~"

Before the package was even opened, Haunter curiously stuck its head inside.

Johan: "…"

Upon opening it, Johan found a TM (Technical Machine) and an invitation letter inside.

He remembered that when the video of his battle with Lance had gone viral, Lance had mentioned compensating him with an item or a TM.

"Nasty Plot?"

Seeing the move inscribed in the TM, Johan's eyes lit up.

[Nasty Plot: Sharply boosts the user's Special Attack.]

"This TM isn't cheap. Lance really went all out… or could it have been given by Agatha?"

"Nasty Plot" is like "Swords Dance", except it boosts Special Attack instead of Physical Attack. Moves that significantly boost stats like these are often more expensive than most high-power moves.

Of course, they're also harder to learn.

"But… both Haunter and Vulpix can learn Nasty Plot."

This is what pleased Johan the most.

Haunter is primarily a Special Attacker, and Vulpix's Special Attack stat is also relatively stronger than its Physical Attack.

It's like getting two moves for the price of one.

Absolute profit!

"Haunter, this challenging task is up to you."

Johan handed the TM to Haunter with a serious expression and instructed it to learn the move together with Vulpix.

"Ka-kah?" Haunter tilted its head.

"You're supposed to be naturally gifted, right? A move like this shouldn't be a problem for you," Johan said with a look of suspicion.

Haunter immediately straightened up and pounded its chest in a show of confidence.

"Ka-kah! ╭(●｀●)╯"

Pfft, easy! Just watch me!

Taking the TM, Haunter turned away with slumped shoulders and sighed.

Maintaining this genius persona is exhausting. Why did I ever pretend to be one?

"Oowu?" Vulpix tilted its head adorably, watching Haunter with wide eyes.

Seeing Vulpix's innocent gaze, Haunter's spirits lifted.

It floated down next to Vulpix and took a deep breath.

Alright, let's do this!

Johan chuckled softly.

This wasn't exactly him pressuring Haunter; it was more like Haunter had dug its own hole and Johan was just going along with it.

He turned his attention to the invitation.

When he opened it, he was surprised to find a ticket inside.

"A VIP ticket for the Youth Exhibition Match?"

Johan couldn't help but be amused.

"Well, at least it's not an invitation to participate."

In the Pokémon world, there are countless tournaments due to the prominence of Pokémon battles, and most of them are quite popular.

From the grand All-League Master Tournament to small town Baby Cups, each has its own audience.

Competitions can generally be divided into three types: Exhibition Matches, Standard Battles, and Free-For-All Matches.

Among them, Free-For-All Matches are slowly phasing out as time goes on.

Standard Battles are the mainstream nowadays.

For instance, the World Championships, Master's Points Tournaments, Gym Leader Challenges, and Specialist Battles all fall into this category.

As for Exhibition Matches, as the name suggests, they're organized by inviting well-known competitors to participate. Winning or losing isn't important; the goal is to be entertaining and put on a good show. The matches neither affect scores nor rankings.

"Since when does Lance participate in exhibition matches?"

However, when Johan saw the sponsors listed…

"Wow, Silph Co., Devon Corp., Pokétime, and the Battle Company… The appearance fees for this must be astronomical."

Typically, major companies sponsor exhibition matches to promote their new products.

But for so many big corporations to join forces…

It was clear that the League was planning something.

"The venue is Sootopolis City in Hoenn?"

Johan rubbed his chin, his mind spinning with several speculations.

First, the League might be planning to formally introduce Pokémon Contests to the stage.

Second, his mind kept flashing back to an old, vague memory—a plan involving capturing Rayquaza!

"Forget it; it's not really my business anyway."

Johan placed the invitation on his shelf.

Whether or not he'd attend depended on his schedule.

After packing up his things, Johan called Haunter and Vulpix, and they left the house.

They took a taxi to the largest electronics market in Rustboro City.

The enormous shopping center housed every piece of equipment available in the Pokémon world, from phones, navigational devices, and Pokétime watches to Berry Blenders and more.

Inside, aside from the wide variety of merchandise, there were also Electric-type Pokémon everywhere.

There were Plusle and Minun running in hamster-like wheels, Elekid twirling their wired arms like power cords, and even an Ampharos holding a tub of popcorn, fixated on a TV drama…

"Ka-kah!" Haunter exclaimed.

It spotted a cute Pichu darting by and became excited.

"Oowu?" Vulpix turned in the direction Haunter pointed, but the golden little creature had already disappeared.

Johan led them to a specialized shop for Berry Blenders.

Despite having done some research beforehand, seeing the price tags in person made him grimace.

Most were tens of thousands of dollars at the minimum.

"Hello, sir. How may I assist you today?" a saleswoman greeted him with a professional smile.

"I'm looking for a second-hand Berry Blender," Johan replied.

"Of course."

The saleswoman didn't show any displeasure at his request for a second-hand machine. In fact, customers coming in to find second-hand blenders were quite common.

"Please follow me."

She led Johan to a storage room filled with various models of Berry Blenders, some visibly older and others relatively newer, with a wide range of prices.

"If you don't mind me asking, are you a Trainer?" she inquired.

"Uh, sort of," Johan responded.

"In that case, you might be interested in this."

She handed him a flyer from a nearby shelf.

"Ka-kah—"

Haunter took the flyer and handed it to Johan, peeking over his shoulder to read along.

"'Sponsored by Rustboro Electronics Mall, the Elekid Cup Trainer Tournament'? The prize is…"

Click!

Suddenly, the lights flickered and went out.

"Ka-kah?"

A power outage?

But the saleswoman seemed unfazed. She called out, "Ampharos, if you would, please."

"Baaa~~"

With a soft hum and a red glow from its tail, Ampharos generated electricity, and the store's lights quickly came back on.

Johan noticed that other stores were also quickly restoring power using their own Electric-type Pokémon.

So, the stores keep Electric Pokémon for power outages?

But the real question was…

How does a major commercial district like Rustboro Mall lose power in the first place?

# 68: Volkner

"Why does an electronics mall have power outages?" Johan couldn't help but ask.

"Ah, well…" the saleswoman sighed, casually waving her hand as if she had grown accustomed to it.

"This has been going on for quite a while. The power goes out every so often, and eventually, we all just started bringing our Electric-type Pokémon here to the mall."

"Has Officer Jenny looked into it?"

"Officer Jenny is investigating, but it's not something that can be solved in a day or two…"

Boom!

Before she could finish speaking, there was a loud bang outside, accompanied by the faint sight of flames.

The sudden noise startled the saleswoman, and the Ampharos generating electricity inside the store also flinched in fright.

"Ka-kah!"

Haunter floated in front of Johan, and in his arms, Vulpix trembled, grabbing tightly onto Johan's clothes.

"Does… this kind of thing happen regularly too?" Johan pointed towards the flames.

The saleswoman swallowed nervously, losing her previous composure. Her voice faltered, "N-not usually, no. This is the first time…"

Boom!

Another explosion echoed.

The entire electronics mall faintly shook, and the floor beneath them seemed to tremble slightly.

That was enough to snap the store employees and customers into action, as they began to rush outside in a panic.

The saleswoman, her Ampharos, and Johan joined the escaping crowd.

Fortunately, the explosion occurred outside the mall and didn't cause severe damage, but it did create quite a bit of fear.

As Johan led Haunter and Vulpix out of the mall, he noticed that Officer Jenny and the Rustboro City police were already on the scene, working to evacuate the area, manage traffic, and cordon off the explosion site.

"It's alright," Johan reassured Vulpix, gently stroking its back.

"Oowu…" Vulpix whimpered softly, but it was braver than before.

"Mr. Johan?"

Officer Jenny spotted Johan in the crowd, and her eyes brightened.

"Officer Jenny."

Johan approached, knowing he couldn't just ignore the greeting. He was also curious about what was going on, so he asked.

Officer Jenny had a serious expression.

"We're not entirely sure about the exact cause, but we suspect it might be related to the frequent power outages at the electronics mall."

"We actually brought in an expert today to try and figure out the cause of the power outages. But just as he arrived, this explosion happened…"

Johan nodded in understanding.

No wonder Officer Jenny's team had arrived so quickly. It wasn't just because they were nearby.

"An expert? Wattson from Mauville City?"

If it's about electricity and circuitry, no one in Hoenn could be more knowledgeable than Wattson.

"It's caused by electrical current interference."

A voice interrupted their conversation.

Turning, Johan saw a young man with golden hair wearing a blue jacket. On his shoulder was a Pikachu, and the man himself seemed somewhat lethargic.

"Minato Namikaze?" Johan's mind jumped, but he quickly corrected himself. "No, Volkner?"

This was Volkner, the Gym Leader from Sunyshore City in Sinnoh!

Johan was genuinely surprised to run into Volkner here in Rustboro City, Hoenn.

"Ka-kah!!"

Seeing the Pikachu on Volkner's shoulder, Haunter's eyes gleamed.

It's Pikachu! Hehe, a real live Pikachu…

Sensing Haunter's intense stare, Volkner's Pikachu tensed up. Tiny arcs of electricity danced across its cheeks in response.

"P-Pikachu!" W-what are you planning?

Johan quickly pulled Haunter back behind him.

At this point, Johan seriously considered cutting Haunter off from watching those "idol" shows.

Being a fan comes at a high cost.

Isn't Vulpix cute enough?

Thankfully, Officer Jenny began to introduce them to each other.

"Allow me to introduce… Volkner, this is Mr. Johan, a Pokémon psychologist."

Volkner's eyes lingered on Johan for a moment.

That name sounds vaguely familiar, Volkner thought.

"And this is Mr. Volkner, the Gym Leader from Sunyshore City in Sinnoh…"

Originally, Officer Jenny's team had planned to reach out to Wattson from Mauville City, but when they couldn't get a hold of him, they ended up contacting Volkner, who happened to be in Mauville City. After learning of the situation, Volkner agreed to come and help out.

Johan suddenly remembered: Wattson was busy with the modernization of New Mauville, wasn't he?

Wattson was working on modernizing New Mauville, and for some of the changes, he had consulted with Volkner.

After all, as the "Lightning of Sunyshore," Volkner was likely involved in the full-scale electrification of Sunyshore City as well.

After brief introductions, Officer Jenny turned to Volkner.

"Mr. Volkner, you mentioned that the explosion was caused by electrical current interference?"

"Yes," Volkner replied, sounding a bit drained. However, when discussing anything related to electricity and technology, he seemed to regain some energy.

"I took a quick look. The explosion came from one of the circuit boxes in the area, and there are clear signs of electrical current interference."

As he spoke, he furrowed his brow.

"Rustboro City's electrical grid isn't that old, and the established circuits shouldn't suddenly fail like this. Furthermore, the conflicting electrical currents seemed more like… those produced by Electric-type Pokémon."

"Electric-type Pokémon?" Officer Jenny repeated in surprise.

Volkner nodded.

He then began explaining in technical terms about using Electric Pokémon for generating and conducting electricity. Neither Officer Jenny nor Johan fully understood everything.

In summary, regular Pokémon wouldn't be able to release such a large amount of electricity all at once.

After all, there are so many Electric-type Pokémon in the world. If any random one could wreck a city's power grid, things would be in chaos.

That's why urban circuits and circuit boxes are specially treated and shouldn't be damaged so easily.

Of course…

Electric-type Pokémon like Electivire, which can supply an entire building's electricity for a year, aren't part of that consideration.

Moreover, for an Electabuzz to evolve into Electivire, it needs an Electirizer and a trade evolution to trigger the transformation.

So far, there haven't been any cases of wild Electivire.

"What if an Electric Pokémon was storing electricity for days or even longer?" Johan asked.

Volkner looked thoughtful.

"In that case, it could be possible. However… Pokémon with that kind of storage capacity are quite rare."

Johan and Officer Jenny exchanged glances.

They shared the same suspicion.

A rampaging Electric-type Pokémon!

However, they couldn't be sure yet.

If it were a rampaging Pokémon, it would probably have caused a bigger scene by now.

"For now, let's restore power here," Johan suggested, and Officer Jenny agreed.

She couldn't help but think to herself—If Ash were here, he could at least act as a resistor…

"This will take some time," Volkner said, retrieving equipment from Officer Jenny's police car. He and Pikachu then headed towards the circuit box where the explosion had been.

Watching Volkner's back, Johan rubbed his chin.

Has Volkner already grown tired of battles?

# 69: Pikachu’s Request

Nearing midday, outside the electronics mall, in a nearby restaurant…

"If there's nothing else, I'll be taking my leave," the restaurant owner said, rubbing his hands nervously and forcing a smile.

Officer Jenny nodded. "Thank you."

Bang!

Before she could finish speaking, the restaurant owner had already slid the door shut and made a quick exit.

Although Volkner was already fixing the power box, no one knew if another explosion might occur or how powerful it might be. If not for Officer Jenny's request, the owner would have been long gone.

"Vulpix, would you mind?" Johan asked, smiling as he handed a cup to the Pokémon.

"Oowu—"

With a gentle breath from the little fox, the drink instantly turned ice-cold.

"Great job!" Johan praised, lightly patting its head.

"Oowu~"

Vulpix let out a soft, pleased cry, its six tails swaying gently, clearly enjoying the compliment.

Seeing this, Officer Jenny and the nearby officers began placing their own cups in front of the little fox.

"Oowu?" Vulpix hesitated and moved closer to Johan, seeking comfort.

"Helping others is a joy," Johan encouraged with a smile.

After a brief moment of hesitation, Vulpix stepped forward and blew on each cup, turning every drink into a chilled delight.

"Logically, I get it, but it's just so cute!"

"Thank you, little Vulpix! You're amazing!"

Hearing all the praise, Vulpix ducked its head shyly, but its tails wagged even more vigorously.

"Mr. Johan, Vulpix is getting more lively by the day," Officer Jenny noted with a smile.

Johan nodded warmly. As he made regular visits to the Rustboro Police Station, Officer Jenny had witnessed the gradual change in Vulpix over time.

For Vulpix, learning to control its cold breath and turn drinks into iced ones was not just training its power but also its confidence, helping it face more unfamiliar people and bask in well-deserved praise.

Meanwhile, Volkner's Pikachu held a plug in its paws, its cheeks sparking gently to keep the rice cooker running.

Haunter floated over stealthily, offering a Mago Berry.

"Ka-kah~~ (Hungry?)"

Pikachu glanced at the berry and turned its head aside.

"Pika~~ (You shouldn't eat food from strangers.)"

"Ka-kah? (Really?)" Haunter took out some Pokéblocks.

"Pika-pika! (Unless you can't resist!)"

Pikachu grabbed the Pokéblock from Haunter and gobbled it down in one bite.

"Pika~chuu!!"

Haunter chuckled mischievously. It had learned a thing or two about winning others over with food from Johan.

With the ice broken, Haunter felt more confident continuing the conversation.

"Ka-kah ka-kah~~"

"Pika pika~~"

"Kakah"

"Pika…"

"…"

For Pokémon, beyond discussing their daily meals, the most significant topic was often their Trainers.

Haunter, of course, couldn't stop singing Johan's praises, talking about his top-notch skills in psychology. He was one of a kind! Well, except for those odd browsing histories that showed up on his phone now and then…

Pikachu wasn't one to be outdone. It declared that Volkner was the best Trainer ever—great at battles and an amazing inventor. But even Pikachu admitted it didn't understand everything that showed up on his devices sometimes.

As the conversation progressed, Pikachu's ears drooped.

Over the past few years, Volkner's enthusiasm for battling had waned. No, it wasn't just waning—he seemed to have developed an aversion to it. For Pikachu, a Pokémon that loved battling, this was tough to adjust to.

"Ka-kah~~ (That's a problem that needs fixing!)"

Haunter rubbed its chin thoughtfully.

"P-Pika? (A problem? How do we fix it?)"

Hearing the word "problem" made Pikachu nervous.

Haunter turned to glance at Johan.

Pikachu followed its gaze.

Him?

Haunter gave a sly grin and patted Pikachu on the shoulder reassuringly.

"Ka-kah~~ (Don't worry. Friend discount.)"

Swish—

The sliding door opened, and Volkner, hands in his pockets, walked in carrying his tools.

"Pika~~"

Pikachu jumped up onto his shoulder.

"Mr. Volkner?" Officer Jenny asked as she stood up.

Volkner raised an eyebrow and spoke lazily, "It's stable for now, but the converter is missing a part. You'll need to replace it with a new one later."

"Understood." Officer Jenny nodded.

After they all sat down, with Pikachu's efforts, the rice was finally cooked, and they began to eat.

During the meal, Volkner spoke up again. "It's likely the work of an Electric-type Pokémon. I found bite marks on some of the wires."

"That's what we suspected," Officer Jenny said, sneaking a glance at Johan. She added, "I've already informed my colleagues back at the station, and they're searching the area."

Volkner simply nodded and continued eating.

"Ka-kah~~"

After the meal, Haunter discreetly tugged on Johan's sleeve and led him aside to explain what had transpired with Pikachu.

"You're serious?"

"Ka-kah!"

"You didn't make any weird requests, like licking it? I have to remind you, Volkner's Pikachu is male…"

"Ka-kah!!ヽ(#`Д)"

Do I look like that kind of ghost?!

Haunter seemed genuinely offended.

Johan glanced over at Volkner, meeting Pikachu's gaze. It blinked at him and Haunter with a knowing look.

Johan sighed, feeling a headache coming on.

He's a Pokémon psychologist, not a psychologist for humans. How can you compare a person's complex mind to a Pokémon's simple one?

And yet…

Why was a Pokémon seeking psychological help for its own Trainer?

More importantly, why was this "client" brought in by his own Pokémon?

Johan decided to just let things unfold naturally.

"We're almost there."

Officer Jenny parked the car near a public park.

They noticed an open manhole cover by the roadside, with burn marks surrounding it.

"Pika!"

Pikachu, sitting on Volkner's shoulder, perked up. It looked toward a wooded area in the park and let out a warning cry.

Electricity crackled faintly across its cheeks.

Suddenly—

Bzzt!

Amidst a harsh buzzing sound, a bolt of golden electricity shot out from the woods.

Without hesitation, Volkner tossed out a Poké Ball.

In a flash of red light, a Luxray, with a body covered in black and blue fur resembling that of a full-grown lion, appeared in front of the incoming lightning bolt.

"Luxray, use Thunder Fang!" Volkner commanded.

Boom!

In an instant, the electric energy exploded, scattering currents that seared a nearby tree, leaving charred marks.

"Awooo!!"

Luxray assumed an aggressive stance, its fur bristling with electricity.

Volkner frowned. He couldn't see their opponent, but Luxray, with its x-ray vision, had already locked onto its target.

After a moment's hesitation, Volkner gave the command: "Luxray, use Thunderbolt!"

"Awooo—"

Electricity surged around Luxray's body, and a bright golden arc shot toward the trees.

Yet surprisingly, there was no reaction, as if the attack had vanished into thin air.

Furrowing his brows deeper, Volkner wasn't satisfied with the result.

"Pikachu, Luxray, use Thunderbolt again!"

Pikachu leapt from his shoulder.

"Pika—chuuu!!"

"Awooo!!"

Two bolts of lightning, like twin golden serpents, struck into the trees with astonishing speed.

But once again, there was no response at all.

What was going on?

Even if the opponent was strong, there should have been some reaction.

Meanwhile, in Johan's field of vision, a Pokédex entry had appeared, revealing the opponent's identity.

His expression became slightly strange, and a serious look appeared in his eyes.

It seemed like this incident might be connected to Vulpix as well.

Because the opponent turned out to be a Vikavolt with… four Charjabug!

Johan stepped forward, surprising Volkner.

"I'll take care of this."

# 70: Vikavolt and Charjabug

"Haunter."

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter chuckled mischievously as it silently faded into the shadows. The dense forest, with its many patches of darkness, provided plenty of places for it to move freely.

Boom!!

Suddenly—

Amidst an eruption of electricity, a Pokémon, resembling a stag beetle with a pair of massive mandibles proportionate to its body, flew out of the dense foliage. Its wings, thin and transparent like those of a cicada, buzzed as it skillfully navigated through the forest.

"A Vikavolt?"

Volkner and Officer Jenny were both caught off guard at the sight of this Pokémon.

"Isn't that an Alolan Pokémon?" Jenny muttered.

As they watched, Vikavolt's abdomen began to glow with a golden current, which quickly gathered between its large mandibles and turned into a powerful stream of electricity, aimed right at Haunter hiding within the forest.

"Ka-kah!!"

Startled, Haunter dived back into the shadows of the trees.

Boom!!

The powerful electric blast struck the ground, creating a sizeable crater and leaving nearby tree trunks and leaves charred from the scorching electricity.

"This Vikavolt… seems to be larger than most," remarked Volkner with a serious expression. Being an expert in Electric-type Pokémon, he could gauge the power behind Vikavolt's previous "Discharge" attack.

"Is it frenzied?" murmured Officer Jenny.

Volkner glanced over with a puzzled expression.

"Frenzied?"

"Not entirely," Johan interjected, shaking his head without elaborating for Volkner. Instead, he pointed to Vikavolt's legs and asked, "Did you see what it's carrying?"

"Charjabug?"

Following Johan's gaze as the golden sparks faded, Volkner's eyes widened in realization.

"Four of them?!"

Charjabug, an Electric and Bug-type Pokémon, looks like a small green rectangle. On closer inspection, one can see that it actually has jointed legs, tiny mandibles, and beady eyes. Charjabug is an apt name because it essentially acts as a mobile battery capable of storing and providing electricity for other Pokémon like Vikavolt.

The occasional blackouts at the electronics mall, as well as Luxray and Pikachu's ineffective attacks earlier, were likely the result of these Charjabug.

In reality, Vikavolt is the evolved form of Charjabug. Many wild Vikavolt carry at least one Charjabug with them, both for recharging and to protect the weaker Pokémon.

But this Vikavolt had four Charjabug!

This wasn't just a matter of having more power. It was due to Charjabug's ability Battery, which increases the power of a partner's special moves by 1.3 times.

With four Charjabug…

Even if it wasn't clear whether the effects of Battery could stack, it was obvious that this Vikavolt not only had ample power reserves but was also a force of destruction!

"They're not behaving normally," Volkner said, his voice heavy with concern.

Being an expert on Electric Pokémon, he could sense that something was off with both Vikavolt and the Charjabug.

"Is this the frenzy you were talking about?" he asked, recalling what Officer Jenny had mentioned earlier.

At the same time, Haunter and Vikavolt's battle was already underway.

Despite having the support of four Charjabug, and possessing high special attack power by nature, Vikavolt's weaknesses were quite apparent.

It lacked speed.

While Vikavolt seemed agile in the forest, its speed was still a major shortcoming, especially now that it was burdened by four Charjabug.

"Haunter, use Toxic."

Johan's calm voice carried into the forest.

"Ka-kah!"

Moving from shadow to shadow, Haunter suddenly appeared behind Vikavolt, letting out a mischievous laugh before spewing a sticky, deep purple substance onto the larger insect.

Bzzzz—

Vikavolt's wings began to hum rapidly as electricity poured from the Charjabug into its body, enveloping it in a golden aura.

In the next moment—

Crackle!!

The air erupted as electricity, like waves, spread violently outward from Vikavolt.

Johan's brows furrowed. He called out, "Dodge, Haunter! Then use Hypnosis!"

"Ka-kah!"

Frightened by the overwhelming display of electricity, Haunter dove behind a nearby tree.

The wild electrical outburst didn't just damage the trees—it even sparked small fires.

"Will Vikavolt even be susceptible to Hypnosis in its current state? That charge of electricity, even if it doesn't create an official Electric Terrain, seems to be producing a similar effect…" Volkner muttered, puzzled.

But Officer Jenny reassured him, "Mr. Johan's Hypnosis is… different."

Different?

"Is that so…" Pikachu's sensitive ears picked up on Alolan Vulpix's soft murmur.

It's Johan's special Hypnosis.

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter emerged from the shadows again, this time directly in front of Vikavolt, with a menacing grin on its face.

It released its grip, allowing a small Hypnosis ring to drop from its paw.

Johan, Officer Jenny, and Volkner all stared in bewilderment.

As the Hypnosis ring swung back and forth, Haunter's eyes gleamed with a red glow, and invisible waves of psychic energy radiated outwards.

Vikavolt's body began to flicker like a faulty light.

Thunk—

One of the Charjabug fell off its legs.

Soon after, the other three Charjabug followed suit, dropping to the ground one by one.

Without the extra power supply, Vikavolt's golden glow dimmed significantly.

"Hex."

Haunter took advantage of Vikavolt's confusion, thrusting its hands forward as dark energy surged from its claws, burrowing into Vikavolt's body.

"Chkaaa!!"

The sharp pain snapped Vikavolt out of its stupor, but with the potent Toxic already taking effect, it quickly began to feel weak.

Still, it fired off a desperate "Thunderbolt"—

But it struck Haunter's Protect instead.

"Use Hypnosis again."

With the Charjabug removed, Vikavolt couldn't maintain the pseudo-electric field effect. Its reduced strength and dwindling stamina made it vulnerable.

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter continued to sway its Hypnosis ring, and when Vikavolt's gaze met its own, a wave of drowsiness crashed through the larger insect's mind.

Its wing movements slowed, and the flickering electric currents gradually faded.

In the end, Vikavolt wobbled before collapsing heavily onto the ground.

Seeing this, Officer Jenny gestured to her officers, and they quickly moved in to put out the fires, capture the Pokémon, and secure the area.

"Ka-kah~~"

Haunter floated out from the woods, patting its chest in a show of mock relief.

That was terrifying for a ghost!

"I remember now," Volkner suddenly spoke up, breaking the silence. He was looking at Johan with a glint of recognition in his eyes.

"You're the Mr. Johan that Aaron mentioned—the one who taught him the Toxic Stall strategy and defeated Lance."

Aaron? Learning Toxic Stall?

Johan was momentarily taken aback as the image of Aaron, a fiery and passionate Trainer, came to mind.

Why would he want to learn Toxic Stall when he's got the strongest friendship combo?
